// src/background.js

const YT_URLS = ["https://www.youtube.com/*", "https://music.youtube.com/*"];

chrome.runtime.onInstalled.addListener(async () => {
  const all = await chrome.storage.local.get(null);
  const oldNpKeys = Object.keys(all).filter((k) => k.startsWith("np_"));
  if (oldNpKeys.length > 0) {
    await chrome.storage.local.remove(oldNpKeys);
  }

  try {
    const tabs = await chrome.tabs.query({ url: YT_URLS });
    for (const tab of tabs) {
      if (!tab.id) continue;
      chrome.scripting
        .executeScript({
          target: { tabId: tab.id },
          files: ["content.js"],
        })
        .catch(() => {});
    }
  } catch {
    // Ignorar si no hay pestañas disponibles
  }
});

chrome.runtime.onMessage.addListener((msg, sender, sendResponse) => {
  if (msg?.type === "GET_TAB_ID") {
    sendResponse({ tabId: sender.tab?.id ?? null });
  }
});

chrome.tabs.onRemoved.addListener((tabId) => {
  chrome.storage.local.remove(`np_${tabId}`).catch(() => {});
});


// ==========================================
// ARCHIVO: C:\Users\christian\Desktop\Project\extensions\yt-recap-extension\src\calendarController.js
// ==========================================

// src/calendarController.js

import { toDateKey } from "./utils.js";

export function bindCalendarControls(dom, uiState, rerender) {
  document.querySelectorAll(".cal-pill[data-calday]").forEach((btn) => {
    btn.addEventListener("click", () => {
      document
        .querySelectorAll(".cal-pill[data-calday]")
        .forEach((b) => b.classList.remove("active"));
      btn.classList.add("active");
      uiState.calDayMode = btn.dataset.calday;
      rerender();
    });
  });

  document.querySelectorAll(".cal-mode-btn[data-calview]").forEach((btn) => {
    btn.addEventListener("click", () => {
      document
        .querySelectorAll(".cal-mode-btn[data-calview]")
        .forEach((b) => b.classList.remove("active"));
      btn.classList.add("active");
      uiState.calViewMode = btn.dataset.calview;
      rerender();
    });
  });

  dom.calSearchInput.addEventListener("input", () => {
    uiState.calSearchQuery = dom.calSearchInput.value;
    rerender();
  });

  dom.calPrevBtn.addEventListener("click", () => {
    uiState.calMonth -= 1;
    if (uiState.calMonth < 0) {
      uiState.calMonth = 11;
      uiState.calYear -= 1;
    }
    uiState.selectedDateKey = toDateKey(
      new Date(uiState.calYear, uiState.calMonth, 1),
    );
    rerender();
  });

  dom.calNextBtn.addEventListener("click", () => {
    uiState.calMonth += 1;
    if (uiState.calMonth > 11) {
      uiState.calMonth = 0;
      uiState.calYear += 1;
    }
    uiState.selectedDateKey = toDateKey(
      new Date(uiState.calYear, uiState.calMonth, 1),
    );
    rerender();
  });

  dom.calTodayBtn.addEventListener("click", () => {
    const today = new Date();
    uiState.calYear = today.getFullYear();
    uiState.calMonth = today.getMonth();
    uiState.selectedDateKey = toDateKey(today);
    rerender();
  });

  dom.calYearPrevBtn.addEventListener("click", () => {
    uiState.calYear -= 1;
    rerender();
  });

  dom.calYearNextBtn.addEventListener("click", () => {
    uiState.calYear += 1;
    rerender();
  });
}

// ARCHIVO: C:\Users\christian\Desktop\Project\extensions\yt-recap-extension\src\calendarView.js

// src/calendarView.js

import {
  translateCategory,
  toDateKey,
  resolveVideoUrl,
  openInstantPlaylist,
  formatClock,
  formatDuration,
  aggregateTracks,
  isMusicItem,
} from "./utils.js";

function getHeatLevel(seconds, hasActivity) {
  if (!hasActivity) return "";
  if (seconds >= 7200) return "heat-4"; // >= 2h
  if (seconds >= 2700) return "heat-3"; // >= 45m
  if (seconds >= 900) return "heat-2"; // >= 15m
  return "heat-1";
}

function getMondayWeeksOfMonth(year, month) {
  const daysInMonth = new Date(year, month + 1, 0).getDate();
  const weeks = [];
  let start = 1;
  let num = 1;

  for (let day = 1; day <= daysInMonth; day++) {
    const dow = new Date(year, month, day).getDay(); // 0 = Domingo
    if (dow === 0 || day === daysInMonth) {
      weeks.push({ num: num++, start, end: day });
      start = day + 1;
    }
  }
  return weeks;
}

function getMonthMetrics(plays, inter, searches) {
  const daysMap = new Map();
  let totalSec = 0;
  for (const p of plays) {
    totalSec += p.seconds || 0;
    const k = toDateKey(new Date(p.timestamp));
    daysMap.set(k, (daysMap.get(k) || 0) + (p.seconds || 0));
  }
  for (const it of inter) {
    const k = toDateKey(new Date(it.timestamp));
    if (!daysMap.has(k)) daysMap.set(k, 0);
  }
  for (const s of searches) {
    const k = toDateKey(new Date(s.timestamp));
    if (!daysMap.has(k)) daysMap.set(k, 0);
  }
  return { totalSec, activeDays: daysMap.size, daysMap };
}

function doesEntryMatchQuery(entry, kind, query) {
  if (!query) return true;
  if (kind === "play") {
    return (
      (entry.title || "").toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
      (entry.artist || "").toLowerCase().includes(query)
    );
  }
  if (kind === "inter") {
    return (
      (entry.title || "").toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
      (entry.artist || "").toLowerCase().includes(query)
    );
  }
  return (entry.query || "").toLowerCase().includes(query);
}

export function renderCalendarView({
  dom,
  uiState,
  getFilteredPlays,
  getFilteredMeta,
  store,
  buildTrackRow,
  renderInteractionRow,
  renderSearchRow,
  onExportPng,
}) {
  const sourcePlays = getFilteredPlays(false);
  const sourceInteractions = getFilteredMeta(store.interactions, false);
  const sourceSearches = getFilteredMeta(store.searches, false);

  const playsByDate = new Map();
  const interByDate = new Map();
  const searchesByDate = new Map();

  for (const item of sourcePlays) {
    const k = toDateKey(new Date(item.timestamp));
    if (!playsByDate.has(k)) playsByDate.set(k, []);
    playsByDate.get(k).push(item);
  }
  for (const it of sourceInteractions) {
    const k = toDateKey(new Date(it.timestamp));
    if (!interByDate.has(k)) interByDate.set(k, []);
    interByDate.get(k).push(it);
  }
  for (const s of sourceSearches) {
    const k = toDateKey(new Date(s.timestamp));
    if (!searchesByDate.has(k)) searchesByDate.set(k, []);
    searchesByDate.get(k).push(s);
  }

  const query = (uiState.calSearchQuery || "").trim().toLowerCase();

  // Mostrar resumen de búsqueda dentro del calendario
  if (query) {
    const matchedPlays = sourcePlays.filter((p) =>
      doesEntryMatchQuery(p, "play", query),
    );
    const matchedDays = new Set(
      matchedPlays.map((p) => toDateKey(new Date(p.timestamp))),
    );
    const matchedSec = matchedPlays.reduce(
      (acc, p) => acc + (p.seconds || 0),
      0,
    );
    dom.calSearchSummary.hidden = false;
    dom.calSearchSummary.textContent = `🔎 «${uiState.calSearchQuery.trim()}»: ${matchedDays.size} días · ${matchedPlays.length} rep. · ${formatDuration(matchedSec)}`;
  } else {
    dom.calSearchSummary.hidden = true;
  }

  // Alternar vista Mes vs Año
  const isYearMode = uiState.calViewMode === "year";
  dom.calMonthView.hidden = isYearMode;
  dom.calYearView.hidden = !isYearMode;

  if (isYearMode) {
    renderAnnualView({
      dom,
      uiState,
      playsByDate,
      interByDate,
      searchesByDate,
      query,
      rerender: () =>
        renderCalendarView({
          dom,
          uiState,
          getFilteredPlays,
          getFilteredMeta,
          store,
          buildTrackRow,
          renderInteractionRow,
          renderSearchRow,
          onExportPng,
        }),
    });
    return;
  }

  // --- VISTA MENSUAL ---
  const monthDate = new Date(uiState.calYear, uiState.calMonth, 1);
  dom.calMonthLabel.textContent = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("es-AR", {
    month: "long",
    year: "numeric",
  }).format(monthDate);

  const rawMonthWord = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("es-AR", {
    month: "long",
  }).format(monthDate);
  const shortMonth = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("es-AR", {
    month: "short",
  }).format(monthDate);
  dom.calWeeksTitle.textContent = `Top por semana de ${rawMonthWord}`;

  // Lunes = 0 ... Domingo = 6
  const firstMondayIdx = (monthDate.getDay() + 6) % 7;
  const daysInMonth = new Date(
    uiState.calYear,
    uiState.calMonth + 1,
    0,
  ).getDate();
  const prevMonthDays = new Date(
    uiState.calYear,
    uiState.calMonth,
    0,
  ).getDate();
  const todayKey = toDateKey(new Date());

  // Calcular rachas visuales dentro del mes actual
  const streakDaysSet = new Set();
  let maxMonthStreak = 0;
  let run = [];
  for (let d = 1; d <= daysInMonth; d++) {
    const k = toDateKey(new Date(uiState.calYear, uiState.calMonth, d));
    const active =
      (playsByDate.get(k)?.length || 0) > 0 ||
      (interByDate.get(k)?.length || 0) > 0;
    if (active) {
      run.push(k);
    } else {
      if (run.length >= 2) run.forEach((rk) => streakDaysSet.add(rk));
      if (run.length > maxMonthStreak) maxMonthStreak = run.length;
      run = [];
    }
  }
  if (run.length >= 2) run.forEach((rk) => streakDaysSet.add(rk));
  if (run.length > maxMonthStreak) maxMonthStreak = run.length;

  const cells = [];
  for (let i = firstMondayIdx - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
    cells.push({
      date: new Date(uiState.calYear, uiState.calMonth - 1, prevMonthDays - i),
      otherMonth: true,
    });
  }
  for (let day = 1; day <= daysInMonth; day++) {
    cells.push({
      date: new Date(uiState.calYear, uiState.calMonth, day),
      otherMonth: false,
    });
  }
  const rem = (7 - (cells.length % 7)) % 7;
  for (let i = 1; i <= rem; i++) {
    cells.push({
      date: new Date(uiState.calYear, uiState.calMonth + 1, i),
      otherMonth: true,
    });
  }

  dom.calGrid.replaceChildren(
    ...cells.map(({ date, otherMonth }) => {
      const key = toDateKey(date);
      const dp = playsByDate.get(key) || [];
      const di = interByDate.get(key) || [];
      const ds = searchesByDate.get(key) || [];
      const daySec = dp.reduce((acc, p) => acc + (p.seconds || 0), 0);
      const hasAny = dp.length > 0 || di.length > 0 || ds.length > 0;

      const btn = document.createElement("button");
      btn.type = "button";
      btn.className = "cal-day";
      btn.textContent = String(date.getDate());

      if (otherMonth) btn.classList.add("other-month");
      if (key === todayKey) btn.classList.add("today");
      if (!otherMonth && streakDaysSet.has(key)) btn.classList.add("in-streak");

      const heat = getHeatLevel(daySec, hasAny);
      if (heat) btn.classList.add(heat);

      const lk = di.filter((x) => x.type === "like").length;
      const sb = di.filter((x) => x.type === "subscribe").length;

      if (hasAny) {
        const tips = [];
        if (dp.length)
          tips.push(`${formatDuration(daySec)} (${dp.length} rep.)`);
        if (lk) tips.push(`❤️ ${lk}`);
        if (sb) tips.push(`🔔 ${sb}`);
        if (ds.length) tips.push(`🔍 ${ds.length}`);
        btn.title = tips.join(" · ");

        if (lk > 0 || sb > 0 || (ds.length > 0 && dp.length === 0)) {
          const dots = document.createElement("span");
          dots.className = "cal-dots";
          if (lk > 0) {
            const d1 = document.createElement("span");
            d1.className = "cal-dot like";
            dots.append(d1);
          }
          if (sb > 0) {
            const d2 = document.createElement("span");
            d2.className = "cal-dot sub";
            dots.append(d2);
          }
          if (ds.length > 0 && dp.length === 0) {
            const d3 = document.createElement("span");
            d3.className = "cal-dot search";
            dots.append(d3);
          }
          btn.append(dots);
        }
      }

      if (query) {
        const dayMatches =
          dp.some((p) => doesEntryMatchQuery(p, "play", query)) ||
          di.some((it) => doesEntryMatchQuery(it, "inter", query)) ||
          ds.some((s) => doesEntryMatchQuery(s, "search", query));
        if (dayMatches) btn.classList.add("match-filter");
        else if (hasAny) btn.classList.add("dimmed");
      }

      if (key === uiState.selectedDateKey) btn.classList.add("selected");

      btn.addEventListener("click", () => {
        uiState.selectedDateKey = key;
        if (otherMonth) {
          uiState.calYear = date.getFullYear();
          uiState.calMonth = date.getMonth();
        }
        renderCalendarView({
          dom,
          uiState,
          getFilteredPlays,
          getFilteredMeta,
          store,
          buildTrackRow,
          renderInteractionRow,
          renderSearchRow,
          onExportPng,
        });
      });

      return btn;
    }),
  );

  // --- DETALLE DEL DÍA SELECCIONADO + RESUMEN RÁPIDO DEL DÍA ---
  const [sy, sm, sd] = uiState.selectedDateKey.split("-").map(Number);
  const formattedDay = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("es-AR", {
    weekday: "long",
    day: "numeric",
    month: "long",
  }).format(new Date(sy, sm - 1, sd));

  const dayPlays = playsByDate.get(uiState.selectedDateKey) || [];
  const dayInter = interByDate.get(uiState.selectedDateKey) || [];
  const dayLikes = dayInter.filter((x) => x.type === "like");
  const daySubs = dayInter.filter((x) => x.type === "subscribe");
  const daySearches = searchesByDate.get(uiState.selectedDateKey) || [];

  const dayTotalSec = dayPlays.reduce((acc, it) => acc + (it.seconds || 0), 0);
  const dayMeta = [];
  if (dayPlays.length)
    dayMeta.push(`${formatDuration(dayTotalSec)} (${dayPlays.length}x)`);
  if (dayLikes.length) dayMeta.push(`❤️ ${dayLikes.length}`);
  if (daySubs.length) dayMeta.push(`🔔 ${daySubs.length}`);
  if (daySearches.length) dayMeta.push(`🔍 ${daySearches.length}`);
  dom.calDayTitle.textContent = dayMeta.length
    ? `${formattedDay} · ${dayMeta.join(" · ")}`
    : formattedDay;

  if (dayPlays.length > 0) {
    const sortedAsc = [...dayPlays].sort((a, b) => a.timestamp - b.timestamp);
    const firstTs = sortedAsc[0].timestamp;
    const lastItem = sortedAsc[sortedAsc.length - 1];
    const lastTs = lastItem.timestamp + (lastItem.seconds || 0) * 1000;
    const tFmt = (t) =>
      new Date(t).toLocaleTimeString([], {
        hour: "2-digit",
        minute: "2-digit",
      });

    const dayCh = new Map();
    const dayCat = new Map();
    for (const p of dayPlays) {
      dayCh.set(
        p.artist || "YouTube",
        (dayCh.get(p.artist || "YouTube") || 0) + (p.seconds || 1),
      );
      const c = p.category || (isMusicItem(p) ? "Music" : "");
      if (c) dayCat.set(c, (dayCat.get(c) || 0) + (p.seconds || 1));
    }
    const topDayCh = [...dayCh.entries()].sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1])[0]?.[0];
    const topDayCat = [...dayCat.entries()].sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1])[0]?.[0];

    const summaryParts = [`🕒 ${tFmt(firstTs)} – ${tFmt(lastTs)}`];
    if (topDayCh) summaryParts.push(`📺 ${topDayCh}`);
    if (topDayCat) summaryParts.push(`🏷️ ${translateCategory(topDayCat)}`);
    dom.calDaySummary.textContent = summaryParts.join(" · ");
    dom.calDaySummary.hidden = false;
  } else {
    dom.calDaySummary.hidden = true;
  }

  const combined = [];
  if (uiState.calDayMode === "all" || uiState.calDayMode === "plays") {
    dayPlays.forEach((d) => {
      if (!query || doesEntryMatchQuery(d, "play", query))
        combined.push({ kind: "play", ts: d.timestamp, d });
    });
  }
  if (uiState.calDayMode === "all" || uiState.calDayMode === "likes") {
    dayLikes.forEach((d) => {
      if (!query || doesEntryMatchQuery(d, "inter", query))
        combined.push({ kind: "like", ts: d.timestamp, d });
    });
  }
  if (uiState.calDayMode === "all" || uiState.calDayMode === "subs") {
    daySubs.forEach((d) => {
      if (!query || doesEntryMatchQuery(d, "inter", query))
        combined.push({ kind: "sub", ts: d.timestamp, d });
    });
  }
  if (uiState.calDayMode === "all" || uiState.calDayMode === "searches") {
    daySearches.forEach((d) => {
      if (!query || doesEntryMatchQuery(d, "search", query))
        combined.push({ kind: "search", ts: d.timestamp, d });
    });
  }
  combined.sort((a, b) => b.ts - a.ts);

  dom.calDayEmpty.hidden = combined.length > 0;
  dom.calDayList.replaceChildren(
    ...combined.map(({ kind, ts, d }) => {
      const timeStr = new Date(ts).toLocaleTimeString([], {
        hour: "2-digit",
        minute: "2-digit",
      });
      if (kind === "play") {
        const sub = `${d.artist} · ${timeStr}${d.isLoop ? " · 🔁 Bucle" : ""}`;
        return buildTrackRow(
          d.isLoop ? "🔁" : "•",
          d.artwork,
          d.title,
          sub,
          d.seconds > 0 ? formatClock(d.seconds) : "Takeout",
          { href: resolveVideoUrl(d) || undefined },
        );
      }
      if (kind === "like" || kind === "sub") return renderInteractionRow(d);
      return renderSearchRow(d);
    }),
  );

  // --- MÉTRICAS DEL MES + COMPARATIVA VS MES ANTERIOR ---
  const inMonth = (y, m) => (item) => {
    const d = new Date(item.timestamp);
    return d.getFullYear() === y && d.getMonth() === m;
  };
  const monthPlays = sourcePlays.filter(
    inMonth(uiState.calYear, uiState.calMonth),
  );
  const monthInter = sourceInteractions
    .filter(inMonth(uiState.calYear, uiState.calMonth))
    .sort((a, b) => b.timestamp - a.timestamp);
  const monthSearches = sourceSearches.filter(
    inMonth(uiState.calYear, uiState.calMonth),
  );

  const prevY = uiState.calMonth === 0 ? uiState.calYear - 1 : uiState.calYear;
  const prevM = uiState.calMonth === 0 ? 11 : uiState.calMonth - 1;
  const prevPlays = sourcePlays.filter(inMonth(prevY, prevM));
  const prevWord = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("es-AR", { month: "short" }).format(
    new Date(prevY, prevM, 1),
  );

  const {
    totalSec: monthTotalSec,
    activeDays,
    daysMap: monthDaysMap,
  } = getMonthMetrics(monthPlays, monthInter, monthSearches);
  const prevTotalSec = prevPlays.reduce((acc, p) => acc + (p.seconds || 0), 0);

  if (monthPlays.length === 0 && monthInter.length === 0) {
    dom.calMonthStatsBox.hidden = true;
  } else {
    dom.calMonthStatsBox.hidden = false;
    const avgSec = activeDays > 0 ? Math.round(monthTotalSec / activeDays) : 0;
    const recordEntry = [...monthDaysMap.entries()].sort(
      (a, b) => b[1] - a[1],
    )[0];
    let recordStr = "—";
    if (recordEntry) {
      const [ry, rm, rd] = recordEntry[0].split("-").map(Number);
      const rFmt = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("es-AR", {
        weekday: "short",
        day: "numeric",
      }).format(new Date(ry, rm - 1, rd));
      recordStr = `${rFmt} (${formatDuration(recordEntry[1])})`;
    }

    const stats = [
      ["Días activos", `${activeDays}/${daysInMonth}`],
      ["Promedio/día", formatDuration(avgSec)],
      [
        "🔥 Racha máx.",
        `${maxMonthStreak} ${maxMonthStreak === 1 ? "día" : "días"}`,
      ],
    ];
    dom.calMonthStatsGrid.replaceChildren(
      ...stats.map(([lbl, val]) => {
        const card = document.createElement("div");
        card.className = "stat-card";
        const sp = document.createElement("span");
        sp.className = "stat-label";
        sp.textContent = lbl;
        const st = document.createElement("strong");
        st.textContent = val;
        card.append(sp, st);
        return card;
      }),
    );

    const diffSec = monthTotalSec - prevTotalSec;
    dom.calMonthCompare.className = "cal-compare-line";
    if (prevTotalSec > 0) {
      const pct = Math.round((diffSec / prevTotalSec) * 100);
      const arrow = diffSec >= 0 ? "▲" : "▼";
      dom.calMonthCompare.classList.add(diffSec >= 0 ? "up" : "down");
      dom.calMonthCompare.textContent = `${arrow} ${diffSec >= 0 ? "+" : "-"}${formatDuration(
        Math.abs(diffSec),
      )} (${pct >= 0 ? "+" : ""}${pct}%) vs ${prevWord} · 🏆 Récord: ${recordStr}`;
      dom.calMonthCompare.hidden = false;
    } else if (recordEntry) {
      dom.calMonthCompare.textContent = `🏆 Día récord del mes: ${recordStr}`;
      dom.calMonthCompare.hidden = false;
    } else {
      dom.calMonthCompare.hidden = true;
    }
  }

  // --- TOP DEL MES ---
  const monthLoops = monthPlays.filter((it) => it.isLoop).length;
  const monthLikes = monthInter.filter((x) => x.type === "like").length;
  const monthSubs = monthInter.filter((x) => x.type === "subscribe").length;

  const mMeta = [];
  if (monthPlays.length)
    mMeta.push(`${formatDuration(monthTotalSec)} (${monthPlays.length}x)`);
  if (monthLoops) mMeta.push(`🔁${monthLoops}`);
  if (monthLikes) mMeta.push(`❤️${monthLikes}`);
  if (monthSubs) mMeta.push(`🔔${monthSubs}`);
  dom.calMonthTopTitle.textContent = mMeta.length
    ? `Top de ${rawMonthWord} · ${mMeta.join(" · ")}`
    : `Top de ${rawMonthWord}`;

  if (monthPlays.length === 0) {
    uiState.monthTopVideoIds = [];
    uiState.monthCardData = null;
    dom.calMonthImgBtn.hidden = true;
    dom.calMonthPlaylistBtn.hidden = true;
    dom.calMonthTopList.replaceChildren();
    dom.calMonthTopEmpty.hidden = false;
    dom.calMonthChannelsBox.hidden = true;
    dom.calMonthCatsBox.hidden = true;
    dom.calWeekdayBox.hidden = true;
  } else {
    dom.calMonthTopEmpty.hidden = true;
    const topMonth = aggregateTracks(monthPlays);
    uiState.monthTopVideoIds = topMonth
      .map((t) => t.videoId)
      .filter(Boolean)
      .slice(0, 20);
    dom.calMonthPlaylistBtn.hidden = uiState.monthTopVideoIds.length === 0;

    const mChannels = new Map();
    const mCats = new Map();
    const weekdaySec = new Array(7).fill(0); // 0=Lun .. 6=Dom
    const weekdayPlays = new Array(7).fill(0);

    for (const it of monthPlays) {
      const ak = it.artist || "YouTube";
      const curCh = mChannels.get(ak) || {
        name: ak,
        plays: 0,
        seconds: 0,
        artwork: it.artwork,
        url: it.channelUrl || "",
      };
      curCh.plays += 1;
      curCh.seconds += it.seconds || 0;
      if (!curCh.artwork && it.artwork) curCh.artwork = it.artwork;
      if (!curCh.url && it.channelUrl) curCh.url = it.channelUrl;
      mChannels.set(ak, curCh);

      const cat = it.category || (isMusicItem(it) ? "Music" : "");
      if (cat) {
        const curC = mCats.get(cat) || { plays: 0, seconds: 0 };
        curC.plays += 1;
        curC.seconds += it.seconds || 0;
        mCats.set(cat, curC);
      }

      const wdIdx = (new Date(it.timestamp).getDay() + 6) % 7;
      weekdaySec[wdIdx] += it.seconds || 0;
      weekdayPlays[wdIdx] += 1;
    }

    const sortedChannels = [...mChannels.values()].sort(
      (a, b) => b.seconds - a.seconds || b.plays - a.plays,
    );
    const topMonthArtist = sortedChannels[0]?.name || "—";

    uiState.monthCardData = {
      subtitle: `${rawMonthWord.toUpperCase()} ${uiState.calYear}`,
      topTracks: topMonth.slice(0, 5),
      totalSeconds: monthTotalSec,
      totalPlays: monthPlays.length,
      totalLoops: monthLoops,
      topArtist: topMonthArtist,
    };
    dom.calMonthImgBtn.hidden = topMonth.length === 0;

    dom.calMonthTopList.replaceChildren(
      ...topMonth.slice(0, 10).map((track, idx) => {
        const mins =
          track.seconds > 0
            ? `${Math.max(1, Math.round(track.seconds / 60))}m`
            : "Imp.";
        const right =
          track.loops > 0
            ? `${track.plays}x (🔁${track.loops}) · ${mins}`
            : `${track.plays}x · ${mins}`;
        return buildTrackRow(
          String(idx + 1),
          track.artwork,
          track.title,
          track.artist,
          right,
          {
            href: track.videoUrl || undefined,
          },
        );
      }),
    );

    // Top canales del mes por duración
    const chWithTime = sortedChannels.filter((c) => c.seconds > 0);
    dom.calMonthChannelsBox.hidden = chWithTime.length === 0;
    if (chWithTime.length > 0) {
      dom.calMonthChannelsTitle.textContent = `⏱️ Top canales de ${rawMonthWord}`;
      const maxChSec = chWithTime[0].seconds;
      dom.calMonthChannelsList.replaceChildren(
        ...chWithTime.slice(0, 5).map((ch, idx) => {
          const share =
            monthTotalSec > 0
              ? Math.round((ch.seconds / monthTotalSec) * 100)
              : 0;
          const barPct =
            maxChSec > 0 ? Math.round((ch.seconds / maxChSec) * 100) : 0;
          return buildTrackRow(
            String(idx + 1),
            ch.artwork,
            ch.name,
            `${ch.plays} rep. · ${share}% del mes`,
            formatDuration(ch.seconds),
            { href: ch.url || undefined, barPct },
          );
        }),
      );
    }

    // Categorías del mes
    const catList = [...mCats.entries()].sort(
      (a, b) => b[1].seconds - a[1].seconds || b[1].plays - a[1].plays,
    );
    dom.calMonthCatsBox.hidden = catList.length === 0;
    if (catList.length > 0) {
      dom.calMonthCatsTitle.textContent = `Categorías de ${rawMonthWord}`;
      const totalCatW = catList.reduce(
        (acc, [, st]) => acc + (st.seconds || st.plays),
        0,
      );
      dom.calMonthCatsList.replaceChildren(
        ...catList.slice(0, 5).map(([cat, st]) => {
          const pct =
            totalCatW > 0
              ? Math.max(
                  4,
                  Math.round(((st.seconds || st.plays) / totalCatW) * 100),
                )
              : 0;
          const row = document.createElement("div");
          row.className = "cat-row";
          const top = document.createElement("div");
          top.className = "cat-row-top";
          const lbl = document.createElement("span");
          lbl.textContent = translateCategory(cat);
          const meta = document.createElement("span");
          meta.textContent = `${pct}% · ${st.plays}x (${formatDuration(st.seconds)})`;
          top.append(lbl, meta);
          const track = document.createElement("div");
          track.className = "cat-track";
          const fill = document.createElement("div");
          fill.className = "cat-fill";
          fill.style.width = `${pct}%`;
          track.append(fill);
          row.append(top, track);
          return row;
        }),
      );
    }

    // Rendimiento Lun-Dom
    dom.calWeekdayBox.hidden = false;
    const wdLabels = ["L", "M", "X", "J", "V", "S", "D"];
    const wdFull = [
      "Lunes",
      "Martes",
      "Miércoles",
      "Jueves",
      "Viernes",
      "Sábado",
      "Domingo",
    ];
    const usePlaysFallback = Math.max(...weekdaySec) === 0;
    const values = usePlaysFallback ? weekdayPlays : weekdaySec;
    const maxWd = Math.max(...values);

    dom.calWeekdayChart.replaceChildren(
      ...values.map((val, i) => {
        const col = document.createElement("div");
        col.className = "wd-col";
        col.title = `${wdFull[i]}: ${formatDuration(weekdaySec[i])} (${weekdayPlays[i]} rep.)`;

        const bar = document.createElement("div");
        bar.className =
          val === 0 ? "wd-bar zero" : val === maxWd ? "wd-bar peak" : "wd-bar";
        bar.style.height =
          val === 0
            ? "4px"
            : `${Math.max(8, Math.round((val / maxWd) * 100))}%`;

        const lbl = document.createElement("span");
        lbl.className = "wd-lbl";
        lbl.textContent = wdLabels[i];

        col.append(bar, lbl);
        return col;
      }),
    );
  }

  dom.calMonthSocialBox.hidden = monthInter.length === 0;
  if (monthInter.length > 0) {
    dom.calMonthSocialTitle.textContent = `Me gusta y suscripciones de ${rawMonthWord}`;
    dom.calMonthSocialList.replaceChildren(
      ...monthInter.slice(0, 20).map(renderInteractionRow),
    );
  }

  // --- SEMANAS REALES (LUN-DOM) + BOTÓN PNG Y PLAYLIST SEMANAL ---
  const weekRanges = getMondayWeeksOfMonth(uiState.calYear, uiState.calMonth);
  dom.calWeeksList.replaceChildren(
    ...weekRanges.map((w) => {
      const wItems = monthPlays.filter((it) => {
        const day = new Date(it.timestamp).getDate();
        return day >= w.start && day <= w.end;
      });
      const card = document.createElement("div");
      card.className = wItems.length === 0 ? "week-card empty" : "week-card";

      const header = document.createElement("div");
      header.className = "week-header";
      const title = document.createElement("span");
      title.className = "week-title";
      const rangeText =
        w.start === w.end
          ? `${w.start} ${shortMonth}`
          : `${w.start}–${w.end} ${shortMonth}`;
      title.textContent = `Semana ${w.num} (${rangeText})`;

      const rightBox = document.createElement("div");
      rightBox.className = "week-right";
      const meta = document.createElement("span");
      meta.className = "week-meta";

      if (wItems.length === 0) {
        meta.textContent = "Sin actividad";
        rightBox.append(meta);
        header.append(title, rightBox);
        card.append(header);
        return card;
      }

      const wSec = wItems.reduce((acc, it) => acc + (it.seconds || 0), 0);
      const wLoops = wItems.filter((it) => it.isLoop).length;
      meta.textContent = `${formatDuration(wSec)} · ${wItems.length} rep.`;
      rightBox.append(meta);

      const topWeek = aggregateTracks(wItems);
      const wArtists = new Map();
      for (const it of wItems) {
        const k = it.artist || "YouTube";
        wArtists.set(k, (wArtists.get(k) || 0) + (it.seconds || 1));
      }
      const topWeekArtist =
        [...wArtists.entries()].sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1])[0]?.[0] || "—";

      const imgBtn = document.createElement("button");
      imgBtn.className = "mini-btn";
      imgBtn.textContent = "🖼️";
      imgBtn.title = "Descargar tarjeta PNG de esta semana";
      imgBtn.addEventListener("click", () =>
        onExportPng(
          {
            subtitle: `SEMANA ${w.num} (${rangeText.toUpperCase()})`,
            topTracks: topWeek.slice(0, 5),
            totalSeconds: wSec,
            totalPlays: wItems.length,
            totalLoops: wLoops,
            topArtist: topWeekArtist,
          },
          imgBtn,
        ),
      );
      rightBox.append(imgBtn);

      const weekIds = topWeek
        .map((t) => t.videoId)
        .filter(Boolean)
        .slice(0, 20);
      if (weekIds.length > 0) {
        const plBtn = document.createElement("button");
        plBtn.className = "mini-btn";
        plBtn.textContent = "▶";
        plBtn.title = "Crear playlist de esta semana";
        plBtn.addEventListener("click", () => openInstantPlaylist(weekIds));
        rightBox.append(plBtn);
      }

      header.append(title, rightBox);

      const ul = document.createElement("ul");
      ul.className = "week-tracks";
      ul.append(
        ...topWeek.slice(0, 3).map((track, idx) => {
          const mins =
            track.seconds > 0
              ? `${Math.max(1, Math.round(track.seconds / 60))}m`
              : "Imp.";
          const right =
            track.loops > 0
              ? `${track.plays}x (🔁${track.loops}) · ${mins}`
              : `${track.plays}x · ${mins}`;
          return buildTrackRow(
            String(idx + 1),
            track.artwork,
            track.title,
            track.artist,
            right,
            {
              href: track.videoUrl || undefined,
            },
          );
        }),
      );
      card.append(header, ul);
      return card;
    }),
  );
}

// --- VISTA ANUAL (365 DÍAS + 12 MESES) ---
function renderAnnualView({
  dom,
  uiState,
  playsByDate,
  interByDate,
  searchesByDate,
  query,
  rerender,
}) {
  const year = uiState.calYear;
  dom.calYearLabel.textContent = String(year);

  const jan1 = new Date(year, 0, 1);
  const dec31 = new Date(year, 11, 31);
  const startPad = (jan1.getDay() + 6) % 7; // Lunes=0

  let yearSec = 0;
  let yearPlays = 0;
  let yearActiveDays = 0;

  const gridNodes = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < startPad; i++) {
    const pad = document.createElement("span");
    pad.className = "yr-cell empty-pad";
    gridNodes.push(pad);
  }

  for (let d = new Date(jan1); d <= dec31; d.setDate(d.getDate() + 1)) {
    const key = toDateKey(d);
    const dp = playsByDate.get(key) || [];
    const di = interByDate.get(key) || [];
    const ds = searchesByDate.get(key) || [];
    const sec = dp.reduce((acc, p) => acc + (p.seconds || 0), 0);
    const hasAny = dp.length > 0 || di.length > 0 || ds.length > 0;

    if (hasAny) yearActiveDays += 1;
    yearSec += sec;
    yearPlays += dp.length;

    const cell = document.createElement("button");
    cell.type = "button";
    cell.className = "yr-cell";
    const heat = getHeatLevel(sec, hasAny);
    if (heat) cell.classList.add(heat);

    if (query) {
      const matches =
        dp.some((p) => doesEntryMatchQuery(p, "play", query)) ||
        di.some((it) => doesEntryMatchQuery(it, "inter", query)) ||
        ds.some((s) => doesEntryMatchQuery(s, "search", query));
      if (matches) cell.classList.add("match-filter");
      else if (hasAny) cell.classList.add("dimmed");
    }

    const labelDate = `${String(d.getDate()).padStart(2, "0")}/${String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0")}/${year}`;
    cell.title = hasAny
      ? `${labelDate} · ${formatDuration(sec)} (${dp.length} rep.)`
      : labelDate;

    const targetMonth = d.getMonth();
    cell.addEventListener("click", () => {
      uiState.calViewMode = "month";
      uiState.calMonth = targetMonth;
      uiState.selectedDateKey = key;
      document.querySelectorAll(".cal-mode-btn").forEach((b) => {
        b.classList.toggle("active", b.dataset.calview === "month");
      });
      rerender();
    });

    gridNodes.push(cell);
  }

  dom.calYearStats.textContent = `${formatDuration(yearSec)} · ${yearPlays} reproducciones · ${yearActiveDays} días activos`;
  dom.calYearGrid.replaceChildren(...gridNodes);
  dom.calYearHeatmapScroll.scrollLeft = dom.calYearHeatmapScroll.scrollWidth;

  // 12 tarjetitas mensuales
  const monthCards = [];
  for (let m = 0; m < 12; m++) {
    const mDate = new Date(year, m, 1);
    const mName = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("es-AR", { month: "long" }).format(
      mDate,
    );
    const days = new Date(year, m + 1, 0).getDate();
    let mSec = 0;
    let mPlays = 0;
    for (let day = 1; day <= days; day++) {
      const k = toDateKey(new Date(year, m, day));
      const dp = playsByDate.get(k) || [];
      mPlays += dp.length;
      mSec += dp.reduce((acc, p) => acc + (p.seconds || 0), 0);
    }

    const card = document.createElement("button");
    card.type = "button";
    card.className = mPlays === 0 ? "yr-month-card empty" : "yr-month-card";

    const title = document.createElement("div");
    title.className = "yr-m-name";
    title.textContent = mName;

    const meta = document.createElement("div");
    meta.className = "yr-m-meta";
    meta.textContent =
      mPlays > 0 ? `${formatDuration(mSec)} · ${mPlays}x` : "Sin datos";

    card.append(title, meta);
    card.addEventListener("click", () => {
      uiState.calViewMode = "month";
      uiState.calMonth = m;
      uiState.selectedDateKey = toDateKey(new Date(year, m, 1));
      document.querySelectorAll(".cal-mode-btn").forEach((b) => {
        b.classList.toggle("active", b.dataset.calview === "month");
      });
      rerender();
    });

    monthCards.push(card);
  }

  dom.calYearMonthsGrid.replaceChildren(...monthCards);
}


// ==========================================
// ARCHIVO: C:\Users\christian\Desktop\Project\extensions\yt-recap-extension\src\cardRenderer.js

// src/cardRenderer.js

import { formatDuration } from "./utils.js";

async function loadImageBitmapSafe(url) {
  if (!url) return null;
  try {
    const res = await fetch(url);
    if (!res.ok) return null;
    return await createImageBitmap(await res.blob());
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

function drawCoverRounded(ctx, bitmap, x, y, w, h, radius) {
  ctx.save();
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.roundRect(x, y, w, h, radius);
  ctx.clip();

  if (bitmap) {
    const { width: sw, height: sh } = bitmap;
    const srcRatio = sw / sh;
    const dstRatio = w / h;
    let sx = 0,
      sy = 0,
      cw = sw,
      ch = sh;
    if (srcRatio > dstRatio) {
      cw = sh * dstRatio;
      sx = (sw - cw) / 2;
    } else {
      ch = sw / dstRatio;
      sy = (sh - ch) / 2;
    }
    ctx.drawImage(bitmap, sx, sy, cw, ch, x, y, w, h);
  } else {
    const g = ctx.createLinearGradient(x, y, x + w, y + h);
    g.addColorStop(0, "#3b1d2e");
    g.addColorStop(1, "#1e2238");
    ctx.fillStyle = g;
    ctx.fillRect(x, y, w, h);
  }
  ctx.restore();
}

function fitCanvasText(ctx, text, maxWidth) {
  const str = String(text || "");
  if (ctx.measureText(str).width <= maxWidth) return str;
  let out = str;
  while (out.length > 1 && ctx.measureText(out + "…").width > maxWidth) {
    out = out.slice(0, -1);
  }
  return out + "…";
}

export async function renderRecapPngBlob(cardData) {
  if (!cardData || cardData.topTracks.length === 0) return null;

  const W = 1080;
  const H = 1350;
  const canvas = document.createElement("canvas");
  canvas.width = W;
  canvas.height = H;
  const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");

  const bg = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, W, H);
  bg.addColorStop(0, "#120c14");
  bg.addColorStop(0.5, "#0d0d12");
  bg.addColorStop(1, "#160e16");
  ctx.fillStyle = bg;
  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);

  const glow1 = ctx.createRadialGradient(180, 180, 20, 180, 180, 460);
  glow1.addColorStop(0, "rgba(255, 78, 69, 0.28)");
  glow1.addColorStop(1, "rgba(255, 78, 69, 0)");
  ctx.fillStyle = glow1;
  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);

  const glow2 = ctx.createRadialGradient(920, 1100, 20, 920, 1100, 520);
  glow2.addColorStop(0, "rgba(255, 153, 204, 0.22)");
  glow2.addColorStop(1, "rgba(255, 153, 204, 0)");
  ctx.fillStyle = glow2;
  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);

  const bitmaps = await Promise.all(
    cardData.topTracks.map((t) => loadImageBitmapSafe(t.artwork)),
  );

  ctx.fillStyle = "#ff85b3";
  ctx.font = "700 26px 'Segoe UI', system-ui, sans-serif";
  ctx.fillText("YOUTUBE & YT MUSIC RECAP", 72, 92);

  ctx.fillStyle = "#ffffff";
  ctx.font = "800 58px 'Segoe UI', system-ui, sans-serif";
  ctx.fillText(fitCanvasText(ctx, cardData.subtitle, W - 144), 72, 162);

  const top1 = cardData.topTracks[0];
  ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.06)";
  ctx.strokeStyle = "rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.12)";
  ctx.lineWidth = 2;
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.roundRect(72, 204, W - 144, 290, 28);
  ctx.fill();
  ctx.stroke();

  drawCoverRounded(ctx, bitmaps[0], 102, 234, 230, 230, 20);

  ctx.fillStyle = "#ff6b63";
  ctx.font = "700 22px 'Segoe UI', system-ui, sans-serif";
  ctx.fillText("#1 MÁS REPRODUCIDO", 362, 276);

  ctx.fillStyle = "#ffffff";
  ctx.font = "700 38px 'Segoe UI', system-ui, sans-serif";
  ctx.fillText(fitCanvasText(ctx, top1.title, 610), 362, 334);

  ctx.fillStyle = "#bbbbcc";
  ctx.font = "500 28px 'Segoe UI', system-ui, sans-serif";
  ctx.fillText(fitCanvasText(ctx, top1.artist, 610), 362, 382);

  const top1Mins =
    top1.seconds > 0
      ? `${Math.max(1, Math.round(top1.seconds / 60))} min`
      : "Importado";
  ctx.fillStyle = "#86efac";
  ctx.font = "600 24px 'Segoe UI', system-ui, sans-serif";
  ctx.fillText(
    `${top1.plays}x reproducciones${top1.loops > 0 ? ` · 🔁 ${top1.loops} bucles` : ""} · ${top1Mins}`,
    362,
    438,
  );

  const kpis = [
    ["TIEMPO TOTAL", formatDuration(cardData.totalSeconds)],
    ["REPRODUCCIONES", String(cardData.totalPlays)],
    ["BUCLES", `🔁 ${cardData.totalLoops}`],
  ];
  const kpiW = (W - 144 - 32) / 3;
  kpis.forEach(([label, val], idx) => {
    const kx = 72 + idx * (kpiW + 16);
    ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.05)";
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.roundRect(kx, 524, kpiW, 118, 20);
    ctx.fill();

    ctx.fillStyle = "#9ca3af";
    ctx.font = "600 20px 'Segoe UI', system-ui, sans-serif";
    ctx.fillText(label, kx + 24, 566);

    ctx.fillStyle = "#ffffff";
    ctx.font = "800 38px 'Segoe UI', system-ui, sans-serif";
    ctx.fillText(val, kx + 24, 616);
  });

  ctx.fillStyle = "#d1d5db";
  ctx.font = "700 26px 'Segoe UI', system-ui, sans-serif";
  ctx.fillText("TOP DEL PERÍODO", 72, 704);

  cardData.topTracks.slice(1, 5).forEach((track, i) => {
    const rowY = 730 + i * 112;
    ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.045)";
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.roundRect(72, rowY, W - 144, 96, 18);
    ctx.fill();

    ctx.fillStyle = "#9ca3af";
    ctx.font = "800 28px 'Segoe UI', system-ui, sans-serif";
    ctx.fillText(String(i + 2), 98, rowY + 58);

    drawCoverRounded(ctx, bitmaps[i + 1], 144, rowY + 14, 68, 68, 12);

    ctx.fillStyle = "#ffffff";
    ctx.font = "700 26px 'Segoe UI', system-ui, sans-serif";
    ctx.fillText(fitCanvasText(ctx, track.title, 580), 232, rowY + 44);

    ctx.fillStyle = "#9ca3af";
    ctx.font = "500 22px 'Segoe UI', system-ui, sans-serif";
    ctx.fillText(fitCanvasText(ctx, track.artist, 580), 232, rowY + 76);

    const mins =
      track.seconds > 0
        ? `${Math.max(1, Math.round(track.seconds / 60))}m`
        : "Imp.";
    ctx.fillStyle = "#e5e7eb";
    ctx.font = "700 22px 'Segoe UI', system-ui, sans-serif";
    ctx.textAlign = "right";
    ctx.fillText(
      `${track.plays}x${track.loops > 0 ? ` (🔁${track.loops})` : ""} · ${mins}`,
      W - 98,
      rowY + 56,
    );
    ctx.textAlign = "left";
  });

  ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.06)";
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.roundRect(72, 1200, W - 144, 86, 20);
  ctx.fill();

  ctx.fillStyle = "#9ca3af";
  ctx.font = "600 20px 'Segoe UI', system-ui, sans-serif";
  ctx.fillText("ARTISTA / CANAL TOP", 100, 1236);

  ctx.fillStyle = "#ff99cc";
  ctx.font = "700 28px 'Segoe UI', system-ui, sans-serif";
  ctx.fillText(fitCanvasText(ctx, cardData.topArtist, 580), 100, 1268);

  ctx.fillStyle = "#6b7280";
  ctx.font = "600 20px 'Segoe UI', system-ui, sans-serif";
  ctx.textAlign = "right";
  ctx.fillText("Tu Recap · Chrome", W - 100, 1252);
  ctx.textAlign = "left";

  bitmaps.forEach((b) => b?.close());

  return new Promise((resolve) => canvas.toBlob(resolve, "image/png"));
}

// ---------- helpers ----------
const toastQueue = [];
let toastBusy = false,
  toastTimer = null,
  toastGapTimer = null;
function toast(msg, isError = false) {
  toastQueue.push({ msg, isError });
  if (!toastBusy) nextToast();
}
function nextToast() {
  const item = toastQueue.shift();
  if (!item) {
    toastBusy = false;
    return;
  }
  toastBusy = true;
  els.toastMsg.textContent = item.msg;
  els.toastIcon.innerHTML = icon(item.isError ? "alert" : "check", 15);
  els.toast.classList.toggle("error", item.isError);
  els.toast.hidden = false;
  toastTimer = setTimeout(() => {
    els.toast.hidden = true;
    toastGapTimer = setTimeout(nextToast, 160);
  }, 3200);
}
const reduceMotion = () =>
  matchMedia("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)").matches;
function el(tag, cls, text) {
  const n = document.createElement(tag);
  if (cls) n.className = cls;
  if (text != null) n.textContent = text;
  return n;
}
function scopeValue() {
  return state.scope === "music" ? "yt_music" : "youtube";
}
function scopeName() {
  return state.scope === "music" ? "Music" : "YouTube sin música";
}
function isMusicScope() {
  return state.scope === "music";
}
function periodPlays() {
  return store.history.filter((it) =>
    matchesFilter(it, state.period, scopeValue(), true),
  );
}
function allPlays() {
  return store.history.filter((it) =>
    matchesFilter(it, "all", scopeValue(), false),
  );
}
function meta(items) {
  return items.filter((it) =>
    matchesPeriodAndSourceOnly(it, state.period, scopeValue(), true),
  );
}
function metaAll(items) {
  return items.filter((it) =>
    matchesPeriodAndSourceOnly(it, "all", scopeValue(), false),
  );
}
function prevPeriodPlays() {
  if (state.period === "all") return [];
  const days = Number(state.period),
    now = Date.now(),
    from = now - days * 86400000;
  return store.history.filter(
    (it) =>
      matchesFilter(it, "all", scopeValue(), false) &&
      it.timestamp >= from - days * 86400000 &&
      it.timestamp < from,
  );
}
function periodLabel() {
  return PERIODS.find(([v]) => v === state.period)?.[1] || "Mi Recap";
}
function openUrl(url) {
  if (url) window.open(url, "_blank", "noopener");
}
function jumpToTab(tabId) {
  const t = npService.tabs.get(tabId);
  if (!t) return;
  chrome.tabs.update(t.id, { active: true });
  chrome.windows.update(t.windowId, { focused: true });
}
async function sendControl(action, value, tabId = state.currentTabId) {
  if (tabId == null) {
    toast("No hay ninguna pestaña de YouTube activa.");
    return false;
  }
  try {
    const res = await chrome.tabs.sendMessage(tabId, {
      type: "RECAP_CONTROL",
      action,
      value,
    });
    if (!res?.ok) {
      toast("Esa acción no está disponible en esta pestaña.");
      return false;
    }
    return true;
  } catch {
    toast("La pestaña ya no está disponible.", true);
    return false;
  }
}
function dedupeSessions(items) {
  const map = new Map();
  for (const it of items) {
    const k = getGroupKey(it);
    const cur = map.get(k);
    if (!cur || it.timestamp > cur.timestamp) map.set(k, it);
  }
  return [...map.values()].sort((a, b) => b.timestamp - a.timestamp);
}
function formatTag(it) {
  if (it.contentType === "short") return "Short";
  if (it.contentType === "live") return "Live";
  if (it.source === "yt_music") return "YT Music";
  if (isMusicItem(it)) return "Música";
  return "";
}
function highlight(text, q) {
  const frag = document.createDocumentFragment();
  const idx = text.toLowerCase().indexOf(q.toLowerCase());
  if (!q || idx < 0) {
    frag.append(text);
    return frag;
  }
  frag.append(text.slice(0, idx));
  const mark = el("mark", null, text.slice(idx, idx + q.length));
  frag.append(mark, text.slice(idx + q.length));
  return frag;
}
function hashStr(s) {
  let h = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < s.length; i++) h = (h * 31 + s.charCodeAt(i)) | 0;
  return h;
}
function playIds(ids, label) {
  const clean = [...new Set(ids.filter(Boolean))].slice(0, 50);
  if (!clean.length) {
    toast(`No hay videos con ID para armar la playlist de ${label}.`);
    return;
  }
  openInstantPlaylist(clean);
}
function cancelCountUps() {
  for (const id of countUps) cancelAnimationFrame(id);
  countUps.clear();
}
function countUp(node, to, fmt) {
  if (!ANIM) {
    node.textContent = fmt(to);
    return;
  }
  const dur = 680,
    t0 = performance.now();
  const step = (t) => {
    const k = Math.min(1, (t - t0) / dur);
    const e = 1 - Math.pow(1 - k, 3);
    node.textContent = fmt(Math.round(to * e));
    if (k < 1) {
      const id = requestAnimationFrame(step);
      countUps.add(id);
    }
  };
  const id = requestAnimationFrame(step);
  countUps.add(id);
}

// ---------- firmas + memo ----------
function computeSigs() {
  const plays = periodPlays(),
    inter = meta(store.interactions),
    searches = meta(store.searches),
    visits = meta(store.channelVisits);
  let sh = 0;
  for (const p of plays)
    sh =
      (sh * 31 +
        hashStr(
          `${p.trackKey}|${p.title}|${p.artist}|${p.contentType}|${p.category}|${p.isMusic ? 1 : 0}`,
        )) |
      0;
  let ih = 0;
  for (const it of inter) ih = (ih * 31 + hashStr(it.id + it.type)) | 0;
  let se = 0;
  for (const s of searches) se = (se * 31 + hashStr(s.id)) | 0;
  let vi = 0;
  for (const v of visits) vi = (vi * 31 + hashStr(v.id)) | 0;
  let struct = `${state.scope}|${state.route}|${state.detail || ""}|${state.period}|${state.peopleSort}|${state.peopleMinSec}|${state.peopleQuery}|${state.likesTab}|${state.hoursMode}|${state.showGhost}|${plays.length}|${sh}|${inter.length}|${ih}|${searches.length}|${se}|${visits.length}|${vi}`;
  if (state.route === "calendar") {
    const buckets = new Map();
    const touch = (k) => {
      if (!buckets.has(k)) buckets.set(k, { sec: 0 });
    };
    for (const p of allPlays()) {
      const k = toDateKey(new Date(p.timestamp));
      const b = buckets.get(k) || { sec: 0 };
      b.sec += p.seconds || 0;
      buckets.set(k, b);
    }
    for (const it of metaAll(store.interactions))
      touch(toDateKey(new Date(it.timestamp)));
    for (const s of metaAll(store.searches))
      touch(toDateKey(new Date(s.timestamp)));
    let hh = 0;
    for (const k of [...buckets.keys()].sort()) {
      const sec = buckets.get(k).sec;
      hh =
        (hh * 31 +
          hashStr(
            `${k}:${sec >= 7200 ? 4 : sec >= 2700 ? 3 : sec >= 900 ? 2 : 1}`,
          )) |
        0;
    }
    struct += `|cal${hh}|${calUi.calYear}|${calUi.calMonth}|${calUi.calViewMode}|${calUi.selectedDateKey}|${calUi.calDayMode}|${calUi.calSearchQuery}`;
  }
  let totalSeconds = 0,
    loops = 0;
  const hours = new Array(24).fill(0);
  for (const p of plays) {
    totalSeconds += p.seconds || 0;
    if (p.isLoop) loops += 1;
    hours[new Date(p.timestamp).getHours()] += p.seconds || 0;
  }
  return {
    struct,
    metrics: `${totalSeconds}|${loops}|${hours.indexOf(Math.max(...hours))}`,
  };
}
function memoVM(tag, fn) {
  const sigKey = `${state.sigs?.struct}|${state.sigs?.metrics}`;
  if (memoSigKey !== sigKey) {
    memo.clear();
    memoSigKey = sigKey;
  }
  if (!memo.has(tag)) memo.set(tag, fn());
  return memo.get(tag);
}
const vmSummary = () => memoVM("summary", () => summaryVM(periodPlays()));

    homeVM(

// ---------- tooltip ----------
function showTip(text, x, y) {
  els.chartTip.textContent = text;
  els.chartTip.hidden = false;
  const pad = 14,
    r = els.chartTip.getBoundingClientRect();
  let left = x + pad,
    top = y + pad;
  if (left + r.width > innerWidth - 8) left = x - r.width - pad;
  if (top + r.height > innerHeight - 8) top = y - r.height - pad;
  els.chartTip.style.left = `${Math.max(8, left)}px`;
  els.chartTip.style.top = `${Math.max(8, top)}px`;
}
function hideTip() {
  els.chartTip.hidden = true;
}
function tipify(node, textFn) {
  node.addEventListener("mousemove", (e) =>
    showTip(textFn(), e.clientX, e.clientY),
  );
  node.addEventListener("mouseleave", hideTip);
}

// ---------- router ----------
function parseHash() {
  const m = location.hash.match(/^#\/(music|youtube)\/([a-z]+)(?:\/(.+))?$/);
  if (m) {
    state.scope = m[1];
    state.route = els.routes[m[2]] ? m[2] : "home";
    state.detail = m[3] ? decodeURIComponent(m[3]) : null;
  }
}
function navigate(scope, route, detail = null) {
  state.scope = scope;
  state.route = route;
  state.detail = detail;
  state.perScopeRoute[scope] = route;
  state.animateNext = true;
  localStorage.setItem("recapFullScope", scope);
  localStorage.setItem(
    scope === "music" ? "recapRouteMusic" : "recapRouteYoutube",
    route,
  );
  const hash = `#/${scope}/${route}${detail ? `/${encodeURIComponent(detail)}` : ""}`;
  if (location.hash !== hash) location.hash = hash;
  else renderAll();
}
window.addEventListener("hashchange", () => {
  parseHash();
  renderAll();
});

// ---------- micro visuales ----------
function sparkArea(values, { w = 320, h = 92, id = "sa" } = {}) {
  const max = Math.max(...values, 1);
  const pts = values.map(
    (v, i) =>
      `${((i / Math.max(1, values.length - 1)) * w).toFixed(1)},${(h - (v / max) * (h - 10) - 4).toFixed(1)}`,
  );
  const last = pts[pts.length - 1].split(",");
  return `<svg viewBox="0 0 ${w} ${h}" preserveAspectRatio="none" aria-hidden="true">
    <defs><linearGradient id="${id}" x1="0" y1="0" x2="0" y2="1">
      <stop offset="0%" stop-color="var(--accent-red)" stop-opacity=".5"/><stop offset="100%" stop-color="var(--accent-red)" stop-opacity="0"/>
    </linearGradient></defs>
    <path d="M0,${h} L${pts.join(" L")} L${w},${h} Z" fill="url(#${id})"/>
    <polyline points="${pts.join(" ")}" fill="none" stroke="var(--accent-pink)" stroke-width="2.5" vector-effect="non-scaling-stroke" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-linecap="round"/>
    <circle cx="${last[0]}" cy="${last[1]}" r="3.5" fill="var(--accent-pink)"/>
  </svg>`;
}
function microBars(values) {
  const max = Math.max(...values, 1);
  const box = el("div", "micro-bars");
  for (const v of values) {
    const i = el("i", v > 0 ? "" : "zero");
    i.style.height = `${Math.max(6, (v / max) * 100)}%`;
    box.append(i);
  }
  return box;
}
function microDots(days7) {
  const box = el("div", "micro-dots");
  for (const d of days7) box.append(el("i", d.active ? "on" : ""));
  return box;
}
function microTicks(hours) {
  const max = Math.max(...hours, 1);
  const peak = hours.indexOf(max);
  const box = el("div", "micro-ticks");
  hours.forEach((v, i) => {
    const t = el("i", i === peak && v > 0 ? "peak" : "");
    t.style.height = `${Math.max(6, (v / max) * 100)}%`;
    box.append(t);
  });
  return box;
}
function microRatio(ratio) {
  const box = el("div", "micro-ratio");
  const fill = el("i");
  fill.style.width = `${Math.round(Math.min(1, ratio) * 100)}%`;
  box.append(fill);
  return box;
}
function microRing(ratio) {
  const r = 12,
    c = 2 * Math.PI * r,
    len = Math.min(1, ratio) * c;
  const box = el("div", "micro-ring");
  box.innerHTML = `<svg viewBox="0 0 30 30"><circle cx="15" cy="15" r="${r}" fill="none" stroke="rgba(255,255,255,.12)" stroke-width="4"/>
    <circle cx="15" cy="15" r="${r}" fill="none" stroke="var(--accent-pink)" stroke-width="4" stroke-linecap="round"
      stroke-dasharray="${len.toFixed(2)} ${(c - len).toFixed(2)}" transform="rotate(-90 15 15)"/></svg>`;
  return box;
}

// ---------- componentes ----------
function kpiCard({
  key,
  iconName,
  label,
  value,
  delta,
  note,
  micro,
  countTo,
  fmt,
}) {
  const card = el("div", "kpi-card");
  if (key) card.dataset.kpi = key;
  const top = el("div", "kpi-top");
  const ico = el("span", "kpi-ico");
  ico.innerHTML = icon(iconName, 15);
  top.append(ico, el("span", "kpi-label", label));
  const val = el("strong", "kpi-value", value);
  card.append(top, val);
  if (countTo != null && fmt) countUp(val, countTo, fmt);
  if (micro) {
    const m = el("div", "kpi-micro");
    m.append(micro);
    card.append(m);
  }
  const foot = el("div", "kpi-foot");
  if (delta != null) {
    const cls = delta > 0 ? "up" : delta < 0 ? "down" : "flat";
    foot.append(
      el(
        "span",
        `kpi-delta ${cls}`,
        `${delta > 0 ? "▲" : delta < 0 ? "▼" : "■"} ${Math.abs(delta)}%`,
      ),
      el("span", "kpi-note", "vs período anterior"),
    );
  } else if (note) foot.append(el("span", "kpi-note", note));
  if (foot.childNodes.length) card.append(foot);
  return card;
}
function kpiStrip(cards, cols) {
  const s = el("div", `kpi-strip${cols ? ` cols-${cols}` : ""}`);
  s.append(...cards);
  return s;
}

function pageHero({
  eyebrow,
  title,
  desc,
  cover,
  visual,
  actions,
  kpis,
  insight,
}) {
  const hero = el("section", `page-hero${visual ? " has-visual" : ""}`);
  const left = el("div", "hero-left");
  if (cover) left.append(cover);
  const text = el("div", "hero-text");
  if (eyebrow) text.append(el("span", "hero-eyebrow", eyebrow));
  const h = el("h2", "hero-title route-title", title);
  h.tabIndex = -1;
  text.append(h);
  if (desc) text.append(el("div", "hero-desc", desc));
  if (actions?.length) {
    const row = el("div", "hero-actions");
    row.append(...actions);
    text.append(row);
  }
  left.append(text);
  hero.append(left);
  if (visual) hero.append(visual);
  const frag = document.createDocumentFragment();
  frag.append(hero);
  if (insight) {
    const chip = el("div", "insight-chip");
    const i = el("span");
    i.innerHTML = icon(insight.icon || "bulb", 16);
    const t = el("span");
    t.innerHTML = insight.text;
    chip.append(i, t);
    frag.append(chip);
  }
  if (kpis?.length)
    frag.append(kpiStrip(kpis, kpis.length <= 4 ? kpis.length : null));
  return frag;
}
function primaryBtn(label, iconName, onClick) {
  const b = el("button", "btn-primary");
  b.type = "button";
  b.innerHTML = icon(iconName, 16);
  b.append(` ${label}`);
  b.addEventListener("click", onClick);
  return b;
}
function ghostBtn(label, iconName, onClick) {
  const b = el("button", "btn-ghost");
  b.type = "button";
  if (iconName) b.innerHTML = icon(iconName, 15);
  b.append(iconName ? ` ${label}` : label);
  b.addEventListener("click", onClick);
  return b;
}
function panel({ title, sub, tools, span = 12, body, iconName }) {
  const p = el("section", `panel span-${span}`);
  const head = el("div", "panel-head");
  if (iconName) {
    const i = el("span", "panel-ico");
    i.innerHTML = icon(iconName, 15);
    head.append(i);
  }
  head.append(el("h3", "panel-title", title));
  if (sub) head.append(el("span", "panel-sub", sub));
  if (tools?.length) {
    const t = el("div", "panel-tools");
    t.append(...tools);
    head.append(t);
  }
  p.append(head);
  const b = el("div", "panel-body");
  if (body) b.append(body);
  p.append(b);
  p._body = b;
  return p;
}
function pillRow(items, active, onPick, ariaLabel) {
  const row = el("div", "pill-row");
  row.setAttribute("role", "group");
  row.setAttribute("aria-label", ariaLabel || "Filtros");
  for (const it of items) {
    const b = el("button", `pill${it.value === active ? " active" : ""}`);
    b.type = "button";
    b.dataset.value = String(it.value);
    b.setAttribute("aria-pressed", String(it.value === active));
    if (it.icon) b.innerHTML = icon(it.icon, 13);
    b.append(it.icon ? ` ${it.label}` : it.label);
    b.addEventListener("click", () => onPick(it.value, b, row));
    row.append(b);
  }
  return row;
}
function setPills(row, value) {
  [...row.children].forEach((b) => {
    const on = b.dataset.value === String(value);
    b.classList.toggle("active", on);
    b.setAttribute("aria-pressed", String(on));
  });
}
function tinyToggle(label, iconName, on, onClick) {
  const b = el("button", `pill tiny${on ? " active" : ""}`);
  b.type = "button";
  b.setAttribute("aria-pressed", String(on));
  b.innerHTML = icon(iconName, 12);
  b.append(` ${label}`);
  b.addEventListener("click", onClick);
  return b;
}
function emptyState(iconName, msg, action) {
  const box = el("div", "empty-cta");
  const i = el("span");
  i.innerHTML = icon(iconName, 26);
  box.append(i, el("p", null, msg));
  if (action) box.append(action);
  return box;
}

function donutChart(segments) {
  const total = segments.reduce((s, x) => s + x.value, 0);
  const wrap = el("div", "donut-wrap");
  if (!total) {
    wrap.append(
      emptyState(
        "chart",
        "Todavía no hay reproducciones en este período para graficar formatos.",
      ),
    );
    return wrap;
  }
  const r = 62,
    c = 2 * Math.PI * r;
  let offset = 0;
  const rings = segments
    .map((s, i) => {
      const len = (s.value / total) * c;
      const out = `<circle class="seg" data-i="${i}" cx="84" cy="84" r="${r}" fill="none" stroke="${s.color}" stroke-width="19" stroke-dasharray="${len.toFixed(2)} ${(c - len).toFixed(2)}" stroke-dashoffset="${(-offset).toFixed(2)}" transform="rotate(-90 84 84)"><title>${s.label}: ${s.value}</title></circle>`;
      offset += len;
      return out;
    })
    .join("");
  const holder = el("div");
  holder.innerHTML = `<svg class="donut" viewBox="0 0 168 168" role="img" aria-label="Distribución de formatos"><circle cx="84" cy="84" r="${r}" fill="none" stroke="var(--surface-inset)" stroke-width="19"/>${rings}<text class="donut-total" x="84" y="80" text-anchor="middle">${total}</text><text class="donut-cap" x="84" y="99" text-anchor="middle">reprod.</text></svg>`;
  const svg = holder.firstChild;
  wrap.append(svg);
  const legend = el("div", "donut-legend");
  const rows = segments.map((s, i) => {
    const row = el("div", "legend-row");
    row.dataset.i = String(i);
    const dot = el("span", "legend-dot");
    dot.style.background = s.color;
    row.append(
      dot,
      el("span", "legend-label", s.label),
      el("span", "legend-val", String(s.value)),
      el("span", "legend-pct", `${Math.round((s.value / total) * 100)}%`),
    );
    legend.append(row);
    return row;
  });
  const hot = (i) => {
    svg.classList.toggle("dim", i != null);
    svg
      .querySelectorAll(".seg")
      .forEach((s) => s.classList.toggle("hot", s.dataset.i === String(i)));
    rows.forEach((r2) =>
      r2.classList.toggle("hot", r2.dataset.i === String(i)),
    );
  };
  svg.querySelectorAll(".seg").forEach((s) => {
    s.addEventListener("mouseenter", () => hot(s.dataset.i));
    s.addEventListener("mouseleave", () => hot(null));
  });
  rows.forEach((r2) => {
    r2.addEventListener("mouseenter", () => hot(r2.dataset.i));
    r2.addEventListener("mouseleave", () => hot(null));
  });
  wrap.append(legend);
  return wrap;
}

function barChart({
  values,
  prev,
  labels,
  fullLabels,
  mode = "bars",
  ghost = false,
  selected = null,
  onPick,
  night = () => false,
  noun = "",
}) {
  const max = Math.max(...values, ...(ghost && prev ? prev : [0]), 1);
  const avg = values.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / values.length;
  const peakIdx = values.indexOf(Math.max(...values));
  const wrap = el("div", `hx${ANIM ? " anim" : ""}`);
  const axis = el("div", "hx-axis");
  for (const f of [1, 0.5, 0])
    axis.append(el("span", null, formatDuration(Math.round(max * f))));
  wrap.append(axis);
  const plot = el("div", "hx-plot");
  for (const f of [0, 0.5, 1]) {
    const g = el("div", "hx-grid");
    g.style.bottom = `${f * 100}%`;
    plot.append(g);
  }
  if (avg > 0) {
    const a = el("div", "hx-avg");
    a.style.bottom = `${(avg / max) * 100}%`;
    a.append(
      el("span", "hx-avg-lbl", `prom. ${formatDuration(Math.round(avg))}`),
    );
    plot.append(a);
  }
  const bars = el("div", `hx-bars${mode === "area" ? " as-hit" : ""}`);
  values.forEach((sec, i) => {
    const cell = el(
      "div",
      `hx-cell${night(i) ? " night" : ""}${i === peakIdx && sec > 0 ? " peak" : ""}${selected === i ? " selected" : ""}`,
    );
    if (ghost && prev && prev[i] > 0) {
      const g = el("div", "hx-ghost");
      g.style.height = `${(prev[i] / max) * 100}%`;
      cell.append(g);
    }
    const bar = el("div", `hx-bar${sec === 0 ? " zero" : ""}`);
    bar.style.height = `${Math.max(2, (sec / max) * 100)}%`;
    cell.append(
      bar,
      el("span", "hx-val", `${fullLabels[i]} · ${formatDuration(sec)}`),
    );
    cell.tabIndex = 0;
    cell.setAttribute("role", "button");
    cell.setAttribute(
      "aria-label",
      `${fullLabels[i]} — ${formatDuration(sec)}${noun ? ` ${noun}` : ""}`,
    );
    const pick = () => onPick?.(i);
    cell.addEventListener("click", pick);
    cell.addEventListener("keydown", (e) => {
      if (e.key === "Enter" || e.key === " ") {
        e.preventDefault();
        pick();
      }
    });
    cell.addEventListener("mousemove", (e) =>
      showTip(
        `${fullLabels[i]} — ${formatDuration(sec)}${ghost && prev ? ` · antes ${formatDuration(prev[i])}` : ""}`,
        e.clientX,
        e.clientY,
      ),
    );
    cell.addEventListener("mouseleave", hideTip);
    bars.append(cell);
  });
  plot.append(bars);
  if (mode === "area") {
    const w = 1000,
      h = 200;
    const pts = values.map(
      (s, i) =>
        `${((i / Math.max(1, values.length - 1)) * w).toFixed(1)},${(h - (s / max) * h).toFixed(1)}`,
    );
    const holder = el("div", "hx-area");
    holder.innerHTML = `<svg viewBox="0 0 ${w} ${h}" preserveAspectRatio="none"><defs><linearGradient id="hxa" x1="0" y1="0" x2="0" y2="1"><stop offset="0%" stop-color="var(--accent-red)" stop-opacity=".55"/><stop offset="100%" stop-color="var(--accent-red)" stop-opacity="0"/></linearGradient></defs><path d="M0,${h} L${pts.join(" L")} L${w},${h} Z" fill="url(#hxa)"/><polyline points="${pts.join(" ")}" fill="none" stroke="var(--accent-pink)" stroke-width="3" vector-effect="non-scaling-stroke" stroke-linejoin="round"/></svg>`;
    plot.append(holder.firstChild);
  }
  if (values[peakIdx] > 0) {
    const ann = el("div", "hx-peak");
    ann.style.left = `${((peakIdx + 0.5) / values.length) * 100}%`;
    ann.innerHTML = icon("trophy", 12);
    ann.append(` ${fullLabels[peakIdx]} · ${formatDuration(values[peakIdx])}`);
    plot.append(ann);
  }
  wrap.append(plot);
  const lbl = el("div", "hx-labels");
  for (const t of labels) lbl.append(el("span", null, t));
  wrap.append(lbl);
  return wrap;
}

function insightsPanel(items) {
  const p = panel({
    iconName: "bulb",
    title: "Insights",
    sub: "lecturas de tu propio historial",
    span: 12,
  });
  if (!items.length) {
    p._body.append(
      emptyState(
        "bulb",
        "Todavía no hay suficiente actividad para generar lecturas.",
      ),
    );
    return p;
  }
  const list = el("div", "insights");
  for (const it of items) {
    const actionable = it.action && it.action.type !== "none";
    const b = el("button", `insight tone-${it.tone}`);
    b.type = "button";
    const i = el("span", "insight-ico");
    i.innerHTML = icon(it.icon, 16);
    const t = el("span", "insight-text");
    t.innerHTML = it.text;
    b.append(i, t);
    if (actionable) {
      const go = el("span", "insight-go");
      go.innerHTML = icon("chevronRight", 14);
      b.append(go);
      b.addEventListener("click", () => runInsight(it.action));
    } else b.disabled = true;
    list.append(b);
  }
  p._body.append(list);
  return p;
}
function runInsight(a) {
  if (a.type === "calendar") {
    if (a.y != null) {
      calUi.calYear = a.y;
      calUi.calMonth = a.m;
      calUi.selectedDateKey = toDateKey(new Date(a.y, a.m, a.d));
      calUi.calViewMode = "month";
    }
    navigate(state.scope, "calendar");
  } else if (a.type === "hour") {
    state.hourFilter = a.h;
    navigate(state.scope, "explore");
  } else if (a.type === "people") navigate(state.scope, "people", a.name);
}

function rankRow({
  rank,
  thumb,
  round,
  name,
  sub,
  value,
  pct,
  tag,
  href,
  onOpen,
  medal,
}) {
  const row = el("button", `pt-row${pct == null ? " no-bar" : ""}`);
  row.type = "button";
  row.append(el("span", `pt-rank${medal ? ` g${medal}` : ""}`, String(rank)));
  let img;
  if (thumb) {
    img = el("img", `pt-thumb${round ? " round" : ""}`);
    img.src = thumb;
    img.alt = "";
    img.loading = "lazy";
    img.decoding = "async";
    attachImgFallback(img, name);
  } else {
    img = el("span", `pt-thumb${round ? " round" : ""}`);
    const mono = createMonogram(name);
    mono.style.fontSize = "15px";
    img.append(mono);
  }
  const metaBox = el("div", "pt-meta");
  const t = el("div", "pt-title", name);
  if (tag) t.append(el("span", "pt-tag", tag));
  metaBox.append(t, el("div", "pt-sub", sub || ""));
  row.append(img, metaBox);
  if (pct != null) {
    const bar = el("div", "pt-bar");
    const fill = el("div", "pt-bar-fill");
    fill.style.width = `${Math.max(2, Math.min(100, pct))}%`;
    bar.append(fill);
    row.append(bar);
  }
  row.append(el("span", "pt-value", value));
  row.title = name;
  row.addEventListener("click", () => {
    if (onOpen) onOpen();
    else openUrl(href);
  });
  return row;
}
function personTile({ name, artwork, meta: m, sub, onOpen, onPlay }) {
  const card = el("button", "person-card");
  card.type = "button";
  const av = el("div", "person-avatar");
  if (artwork) {
    const img = el("img");
    img.src = artwork;
    img.alt = "";
    img.loading = "lazy";
    img.decoding = "async";
    attachImgFallback(img, name);
    av.append(img);
  } else av.append(createMonogram(name));
  if (onPlay) {
    const p = el("span", "person-play");
    p.innerHTML = icon("play", 15);
    p.setAttribute("aria-hidden", "true");
    av.append(p);
  }
  card.append(av, el("div", "person-name", name));
  if (m) card.append(el("div", "person-meta", m));
  if (sub) card.append(el("div", "person-sub", sub));
  card.title = name;
  card.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
    if (onPlay && e.target.closest(".person-play")) {
      onPlay();
      return;
    }
    onOpen();
  });
  return card;
}
function directoryGrid(items, makeTile, batch = 24) {
  const wrap = el("div");
  const grid = el("div", "directory-grid");
  let shown = 0;
  const more = el("button", "grid-more");
  more.type = "button";
  const paint = () => {
    const next = items.slice(shown, shown + batch);
    grid.append(...next.map(makeTile));
    shown += next.length;
    if (shown >= items.length) more.remove();
    else more.textContent = `Mostrar más (${items.length - shown})`;
  };
  more.addEventListener("click", paint);
  paint();
  wrap.append(grid);
  if (shown < items.length) wrap.append(more);
  return wrap;
}

// ---------- hero visuals ----------
function heroVisualExplore() {
  const ins = vmInsights(),
    sum = ins.sum;
  const v = el("button", "hero-visual");
  v.type = "button";
  const total = el("div", "hv-total");
  total.append(
    el("strong", null, sum.empty ? "0 min" : formatDuration(sum.totalSeconds)),
    el("span", null, "últimos 14 días"),
  );
  const chart = el("div", "hv-chart");
  chart.innerHTML = sparkArea(ins.trend, { id: "hvsa" });
  const covers = el("div", "hv-covers");
  for (const t of sum.empty ? [] : sum.topTracks.slice(0, 4)) {
    if (t.artwork) {
      const img = el("img");
      img.src = t.artwork;
      img.alt = "";
      img.loading = "lazy";
      attachImgFallback(img, t.artist);
      covers.append(img);
    } else {
      const mo = createMonogram(t.artist || t.title);
      covers.append(mo);
    }
  }
  v.append(total, chart, covers);
  v.title = "Ver el mes más fuerte en el calendario";
  v.addEventListener("click", () => {
    const best = vmHome()
      .months.filter((m) => m.plays > 0)
      .sort((a, b) => b.seconds - a.seconds)[0];
    if (best) {
      calUi.calYear = best.y;
      calUi.calMonth = best.m;
      calUi.selectedDateKey = toDateKey(new Date(best.y, best.m, 1));
      calUi.calViewMode = "month";
    }
    navigate(state.scope, "calendar");
  });
  return v;
}
function heroVisualCalendar() {
  const heat = vmYearHeat();
  const v = el("button", "hero-visual hv-heat");
  v.type = "button";
  const cols = Math.ceil(heat.cells.length / 7);
  const size = 7,
    gap = 2,
    w = cols * (size + gap),
    h = 7 * (size + gap);
  const colors = [
    "var(--surface-inset)",
    "#15803d",
    "#16a34a",
    "#22c55e",
    "#4ade80",
  ];
  const rects = heat.cells
    .map((c, i) => {
      if (!c) return "";
      const col = Math.floor(i / 7),
        row = i % 7;
      return `<rect x="${col * (size + gap)}" y="${row * (size + gap)}" width="${size}" height="${size}" rx="1.5" fill="${colors[c.level]}"><title>${c.key}</title></rect>`;
    })
    .join("");
  const holder = el("div");
  holder.innerHTML = `<svg viewBox="0 0 ${w} ${h}" role="img" aria-label="Mapa de calor del año">${rects}</svg>`;
  const cap = el("div", "hv-heat-cap");
  cap.append(
    el("span", null, `${calUi.calYear}`),
    el("span", null, `${formatDuration(heat.total)} · clic para vista anual`),
  );
  v.append(holder.firstChild, cap);
  v.addEventListener("click", () => {
    calUi.calViewMode = "year";
    syncCalModeButtons();
    state.sigs = computeSigs();
    renderCalendarRoute();
  });
  return v;
}
function heroVisualPeople(list) {
  const v = el("div", "hero-visual");
  const cap = el("div", "hv-heat-cap");
  cap.append(
    el("span", null, isMusicScope() ? "Tus artistas" : "Tus canales"),
    el("span", null, `${list.length} en el período`),
  );
  const grid = el("div", "hv-collage");
  for (const a of list.slice(0, 6)) {
    const cell = el("div", "hv-av");
    const art = a.artwork || a.avatar;
    if (art) {
      const img = el("img");
      img.src = art;
      img.alt = "";
      img.loading = "lazy";
      attachImgFallback(img, a.name);
      cell.append(img);
    } else cell.append(createMonogram(a.name));
    grid.append(cell);
  }
  v.append(cap, grid);
  return v;
}

// ---------- shell ----------
function renderInlineStats(sum) {
  els.inlineStats.replaceChildren();
  if (!sum.empty) {
    const s = (t) => el("strong", null, t);
    els.inlineStats.append(
      s(String(sum.totalPlays)),
      " rep · ",
      s(formatDuration(sum.totalSeconds)),
      " · ",
      s(String(sum.unique)),
      " únicos",
    );
  } else els.inlineStats.textContent = "Sin datos en este período";
}
function renderShell() {
  document.body.dataset.scope = state.scope;
  document.title =
    state.scope === "music" ? "Recap · Music" : "Recap · YouTube";
  els.brandWord.textContent = state.scope === "music" ? "Music" : "YouTube";
  els.brandLogo.innerHTML = icon(
    state.scope === "music" ? "music" : "youtube",
    16,
  );
  els.brandBtn.title =
    state.scope === "music"
      ? "Cambiar a YouTube sin música"
      : "Cambiar a Music";
  els.brandBtn.setAttribute("aria-label", els.brandBtn.title);
  els.peopleLabel.textContent =
    state.scope === "music" ? "Artistas" : "Canales";
  els.sideNav.querySelectorAll(".side-link").forEach((b) => {
    const active = b.dataset.route === state.route;
    b.classList.toggle("active", active);
    if (active) b.setAttribute("aria-current", "page");
    else b.removeAttribute("aria-current");
  });
  els.periodChips.replaceChildren(
    ...PERIODS.map(([value, label]) => {
      const b = el(
        "button",
        `period-chip${state.period === value ? " active" : ""}`,
        label,
      );
      b.type = "button";
      b.addEventListener("click", () => {
        state.period = value;
        state.animateNext = true;
        localStorage.setItem("recapFullPeriod", value);
        renderAll();
      });
      return b;
    }),
  );
  renderInlineStats(vmSummary());
  renderSideLists();
}
function renderSideLists() {
  const vm = vmHome(),
    nodes = [];
  const liked = meta(store.interactions).filter((it) => it.type === "like");
  const likedBtn = el("button", "side-list");
  likedBtn.type = "button";
  const lt = el("strong");
  lt.innerHTML = icon("heartFill", 12);
  lt.append(isMusicScope() ? " Liked Music" : " Me gusta (videos)");
  likedBtn.append(
    lt,
    el("span", null, `${liked.length} me gusta · lista generada`),
  );
  likedBtn.addEventListener("click", () => navigate(state.scope, "likes"));
  nodes.push(likedBtn);
  for (const m of vm.months.slice(0, 8)) {
    if (!m.plays) continue;
    const b = el("button", "side-list");
    b.type = "button";
    const s = el(
      "span",
      null,
      `${formatDuration(m.seconds)} · ${m.plays} rep.`,
    );
    s.dataset.month = `${m.y}-${m.m}`;
    b.append(
      el("strong", null, `${m.label} Recap '${String(m.year).slice(2)}`),
      s,
    );
    b.addEventListener("click", () => {
      calUi.calYear = m.y;
      calUi.calMonth = m.m;
      calUi.selectedDateKey = toDateKey(new Date(m.y, m.m, 1));
      calUi.calViewMode = "month";
      navigate(state.scope, "calendar");
    });
    nodes.push(b);
  }
  els.sideLists.replaceChildren(...nodes);
}
function updateMetricsInPlace() {
  const sum = vmSummary();
  renderInlineStats(sum);
  const kpis = els.routes.home.querySelectorAll(".hero-kpi strong");
  if (kpis.length === 4) {
    kpis[0].textContent = sum.empty
      ? "0 min"
      : formatDuration(sum.totalSeconds);
    kpis[1].textContent = String(sum.empty ? 0 : sum.totalPlays);
    kpis[2].textContent = String(sum.empty ? 0 : sum.unique);
    kpis[3].textContent = String(sum.empty ? 0 : sum.streak);
  }
  const values = {
    seconds: sum.empty ? "0 min" : formatDuration(sum.totalSeconds),
    plays: String(sum.empty ? 0 : sum.totalPlays),
    unique: String(sum.empty ? 0 : sum.unique),
    streak: `${sum.empty ? 0 : sum.streak} días`,
    peak: sum.empty ? "—" : sum.peakLabel,
    loops: String(sum.empty ? 0 : sum.totalLoops),
  };
  if (state.route === "people" && !state.detail) {
    const t = vmDirectory().totals;
    values.dirSeconds = formatDuration(t.seconds || 0);
    values.dirPlays = String(t.plays || 0);
  }
  if (state.route === "likes")
    values.likedSeconds = formatDuration(vmLikesPage().totals.seconds);
  document.querySelectorAll("[data-kpi]").forEach((card) => {
    const v = values[card.dataset.kpi],
      node = card.querySelector(".kpi-value");
    if (node && v != null) node.textContent = v;
  });
  const months = vmHome().months;
  els.sideLists.querySelectorAll("[data-month]").forEach((sp) => {
    const m = months.find((x) => `${x.y}-${x.m}` === sp.dataset.month);
    if (m) sp.textContent = `${formatDuration(m.seconds)} · ${m.plays} rep.`;
  });
}

// ---------- cards / shelves ----------
function makeCard(opts) {
  const {
    kind = "square",
    artwork,
    title,
    sub,
    stat,
    href,
    onOpen,
    month,
    badge,
    tag,
    name,
  } = opts;
  const card = el("button", "card");
  card.type = "button";
  const cover = el(
    "div",
    "card-cover" +
      (kind === "wide" ? " wide" : "") +
      (kind === "round" ? " round" : "") +
      (kind === "month" ? " month" : ""),
  );
  if (kind === "month")
    cover.append(
      el("span", "m-name", month.label),
      el("span", "m-year", `${month.year} · ${month.plays} rep.`),
    );
  else if (artwork) {
    const img = el("img");
    img.src = artwork;
    img.alt = "";
    img.loading = "lazy";
    img.decoding = "async";
    attachImgFallback(img, name || title);
    cover.append(img);
  } else if (kind === "round") cover.append(createMonogram(name || title));
  else {
    const na = el("span", "no-art");
    na.innerHTML = icon(isMusicScope() ? "music" : "youtube", 34);
    cover.append(na);
  }
  if (tag) cover.append(el("span", "cover-tag", tag));
  if (badge) cover.append(el("span", "cover-badge", badge));
  if (href || onOpen) {
    const p = el("span", "card-play");
    p.setAttribute("aria-hidden", "true");
    p.innerHTML = icon("play", 16);
    cover.append(p);
  }
  card.append(
    cover,
    el("div", "card-title", title),
    el("div", "card-sub", sub || ""),
  );
  if (stat) card.append(el("div", "card-stat", stat));
  card.title = title;
  card.addEventListener("click", () => {
    if (onOpen) onOpen();
    else openUrl(href);
  });
  return card;
}
function skeletonRow() {
  const nodes = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
    const c = el("div", "skel-card");
    c.append(
      el("div", "skel-cover"),
      el("div", "skel-line"),
      el("div", "skel-line short"),
    );
    nodes.push(c);
  }
  return nodes;
}
function enableDrag(row) {
  let down = false,
    dragged = false,
    startX = 0,
    startScroll = 0;
  row.addEventListener("pointerdown", (e) => {
    if (e.button !== 0) return;
    down = true;
    dragged = false;
    startX = e.clientX;
    startScroll = row.scrollLeft;
  });
  row.addEventListener("pointermove", (e) => {
    if (!down) return;
    const dx = e.clientX - startX;
    if (!dragged && Math.abs(dx) > 6) {
      dragged = true;
      row.classList.add("dragging");
      row.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);
    }
    if (dragged) row.scrollLeft = startScroll - dx;
  });
  const end = () => {
    down = false;
    row.classList.remove("dragging");
  };
  row.addEventListener("pointerup", end);
  row.addEventListener("pointercancel", end);
  row.addEventListener(
    "click",
    (e) => {
      if (dragged) {
        e.stopPropagation();
        e.preventDefault();
        dragged = false;
      }
    },
    true,
  );
}
function shelf({ eyebrow, title, cardsFactory, moreRoute }) {
  const key = `${state.scope}|${state.route}|${state.detail || ""}|${state.period}|${title}`;
  const sec = el("section", "shelf");
  const head = el("div", "shelf-head");
  if (eyebrow) head.append(el("span", "shelf-eyebrow", eyebrow));
  head.append(el("h2", "shelf-title", title));
  const tools = el("div", "shelf-tools");
  if (moreRoute) {
    const more = el("button", "shelf-more", "Ver todo");
    more.type = "button";
    more.addEventListener("click", () => navigate(state.scope, moreRoute));
    tools.append(more);
  }
  const prev = el("button", "shelf-arrow");
  prev.type = "button";
  prev.setAttribute("aria-label", "Desplazar a la izquierda");
  prev.innerHTML = icon("chevronLeft", 16);
  const next = el("button", "shelf-arrow");
  next.type = "button";
  next.setAttribute("aria-label", "Desplazar a la derecha");
  next.innerHTML = icon("chevronRight", 16);
  tools.append(prev, next);
