let statusTimer = null;
function showStatus(msg, isError = false) {
  dom.statusBanner.textContent = msg;
  dom.statusBanner.classList.toggle("error", isError);
  dom.statusBanner.hidden = false;
  if (statusTimer) clearTimeout(statusTimer);
  statusTimer = setTimeout(() => {
    dom.statusBanner.hidden = true;
  }, 6000);
}

function renderNowPlaying(np) {
  if (!np) {
    dom.npEmpty.hidden = false;
    dom.npContent.hidden = true;
    return;
  }
  dom.npEmpty.hidden = true;
  dom.npContent.hidden = false;
  if (np.artwork) dom.npThumb.src = np.artwork;
  else dom.npThumb.removeAttribute("src");
  dom.npStatusBadge.textContent = np.paused ? "⏸ En pausa" : "● En vivo";
  dom.npStatusBadge.classList.toggle("paused", np.paused);
  dom.npSourceBadge.textContent =
    np.source === "yt_music"
      ? "YT Music"
      : np.isMusic
        ? "YouTube · Música"
        : "YouTube";
  if (np.category) {
    dom.npCategoryBadge.textContent = translateCategory(np.category);
    dom.npCategoryBadge.hidden = false;
  } else {
    dom.npCategoryBadge.hidden = true;
  }
  const fmtLabels = { short: "Short", live: "En vivo", mix: "Mix" };
  const fmtText =
    fmtLabels[np.contentType] || (np.context === "mix" ? "Mix" : "");
  dom.npFormatBadge.hidden = !fmtText;
  if (fmtText) dom.npFormatBadge.textContent = fmtText;
  dom.npLoopBadge.hidden = !(np.loopActive || np.isLoopSession);
  dom.npTitle.textContent = np.title;
  dom.npArtist.textContent = np.album
    ? `${np.artist} · ${np.album}`
    : np.artist;
  const pct =
    np.duration > 0 ? Math.min(100, (np.currentTime / np.duration) * 100) : 0;
  dom.npBarFill.style.width = `${pct.toFixed(1)}%`;
  const extras = [];
  if (np.playbackRate && np.playbackRate !== 1)
    extras.push(`${np.playbackRate}x`);
  if (np.muted) extras.push("🔇");
  const clock =
    np.duration > 0
      ? `${formatClock(np.currentTime)} / ${formatClock(np.duration)}`
      : formatClock(np.currentTime);
  dom.npTime.textContent = extras.length
    ? `${clock} · ${extras.join(" ")}`
    : clock;
  const remaining = Math.max(
    0,
    (np.minRequired || 20) - (np.sessionSeconds || 0),
  );
  if (remaining === 0) {
    dom.npCountState.textContent = "✓ Registrado";
    dom.npCountState.classList.add("saved");
  } else {
    dom.npCountState.textContent = `Cuenta en ${remaining}s`;
    dom.npCountState.classList.remove("saved");
  }
}

function renderOtherOpenTabs(others) {
  if (others.length === 0) {
    dom.npOtherWrap.hidden = true;
    dom.npOtherList.replaceChildren();
    return;
  }
  dom.npOtherWrap.hidden = false;
  dom.npOtherHeader.textContent = `Otras pestañas abiertas (${others.length})`;
  dom.npOtherList.replaceChildren(
    ...others.map((tab) => {
      const li = document.createElement("li");
      const btn = document.createElement("button");
      btn.type = "button";
      btn.className = "np-other-btn";
      btn.title = "Clic para ver en la tarjeta principal";
      const st = document.createElement("span");
      st.className = tab.paused ? "np-other-state" : "np-other-state playing";
      st.textContent = tab.paused ? "⏸" : "▶";
      const img = document.createElement("img");
      img.className = "np-other-thumb";
      if (tab.artwork) img.src = tab.artwork;
      img.alt = "";
      const meta = document.createElement("div");
      meta.className = "meta";
      const title = document.createElement("div");
      title.className = "np-other-title";
      title.textContent = tab.title;
      const srcLabel =
        tab.source === "yt_music"
          ? "YT Music"
          : tab.isMusic
            ? "YouTube · Música"
            : "YouTube";
      const sub = document.createElement("div");
      sub.className = "np-other-sub";
      sub.textContent = `${srcLabel} · ${tab.artist}`;
      meta.append(title, sub);
      const time = document.createElement("span");
      time.className = "np-other-time";
      time.textContent =
        tab.duration > 0
          ? `${formatClock(tab.currentTime)} / ${formatClock(tab.duration)}`
          : formatClock(tab.currentTime);
      const children = [st, img, meta, time];
      if (tab.tabId != null && npService.tabs.has(tab.tabId)) {
        const jumpBtn = document.createElement("span");
        jumpBtn.className = "np-other-time";
        jumpBtn.textContent = "↗";
        jumpBtn.title = "Ir a esta pestaña en Chrome";
        jumpBtn.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
          e.stopPropagation();
          const realTab = npService.tabs.get(tab.tabId);
          if (realTab) {
            chrome.tabs.update(realTab.id, { active: true });
            chrome.windows.update(realTab.windowId, { focused: true });
          }
        });
        children.push(jumpBtn);
      }
      btn.append(...children);
      btn.addEventListener("click", () => {
        uiState.pinnedNpTabId = tab.storageKey || tab.tabId;
        renderBestNowPlaying();
      });
      li.append(btn);
      return li;
    }),
  );
}

function renderBestNowPlaying() {
  const sel = npService.select(uiState.pinnedNpTabId);
  if (!sel) {
    uiState.pinnedNpTabId = null;
    renderNowPlaying(null);
    renderOtherOpenTabs([]);
    return;
  }
  renderNowPlaying(sel.primary);
  renderOtherOpenTabs(sel.others);
}

function getFilteredPlays(checkDate = true) {
  return store.history.filter((it) =>
    matchesFilter(
      it,
      dom.periodSelect.value,
      dom.sourceSelect.value,
      checkDate,
    ),
  );
}
function getFilteredMeta(items, checkDate = true) {
  return items.filter((it) =>
    matchesPeriodAndSourceOnly(
      it,
      dom.periodSelect.value,
      dom.sourceSelect.value,
      checkDate,
    ),
  );
}

function renderSummaryTab() {
  const vm = summaryVM(getFilteredPlays(true));
  if (vm.empty) {
    dom.totalTime.textContent = "0 min";
    dom.totalPlays.textContent = "0";
    dom.totalLoops.textContent = "🔁 0";
    dom.uniqueCount.textContent = "0";
    dom.streakDays.textContent = "0 días";
    dom.peakHour.textContent = "—";
    dom.exportImgBtn.hidden = true;
    dom.makePlaylistBtn.hidden = true;
    uiState.summaryCardData = null;
    dom.topList.replaceChildren();
    dom.emptyMsg.hidden = false;
    return;
  }
  dom.emptyMsg.hidden = true;
  uiState.topVideoIds = vm.topTracks
    .map((t) => t.videoId)
    .filter(Boolean)
    .slice(0, 20);
  dom.makePlaylistBtn.hidden = uiState.topVideoIds.length === 0;
  const periodLabel =
    dom.periodSelect.options[dom.periodSelect.selectedIndex]?.text ||
    "Mi Recap";
  uiState.summaryCardData = {
    subtitle: periodLabel.toUpperCase(),
    topTracks: vm.topTracks.slice(0, 5),
    totalSeconds: vm.totalSeconds,
    totalPlays: vm.totalPlays,
    totalLoops: vm.totalLoops,
    topArtist: vm.topArtist,
  };
  dom.exportImgBtn.hidden = vm.topTracks.length === 0;
  dom.totalTime.textContent = formatDuration(vm.totalSeconds);
  dom.totalPlays.textContent = String(vm.totalPlays);
  dom.totalLoops.textContent = `🔁 ${vm.totalLoops}`;
  dom.uniqueCount.textContent = String(vm.unique);
  dom.streakDays.textContent = `${vm.streak} ${vm.streak === 1 ? "día" : "días"}`;
  dom.peakHour.textContent = vm.peakLabel;
  dom.topList.replaceChildren(
    ...vm.topTracks.slice(0, 15).map((track, idx) => {
      const mins =
        track.seconds > 0
          ? `${Math.max(1, Math.round(track.seconds / 60))}m`
          : "Imp.";
      const avgPct =
        track.pctCount > 0
          ? ` · ${Math.round(track.pctSum / track.pctCount)}%`
          : "";
      const right =
        track.loops > 0
          ? `${track.plays}x (🔁${track.loops}) · ${mins}`
          : `${track.plays}x · ${mins}${avgPct}`;
      return buildTrackRow(
        String(idx + 1),
        track.artwork,
        track.title,
        track.artist,
        right,
        { href: track.videoUrl || undefined },
      );
    }),
  );
}

function renderRecentTab() {
  if (uiState.recentMode === "plays") {
    const filtered = getFilteredPlays(true);
    dom.recentEmpty.hidden = filtered.length > 0;
    if (filtered.length === 0) {
      dom.recentList.replaceChildren();
      return;
    }
    const items = [...filtered]
      .sort((a, b) => b.timestamp - a.timestamp)
      .slice(0, 30);
    dom.recentList.replaceChildren(
      ...items.map((item) => {
        const timeStr = new Date(item.timestamp).toLocaleTimeString([], {
          hour: "2-digit",
          minute: "2-digit",
        });
        const tags = [];
        if (item.isLoop) tags.push("🔁 Bucle");
        if (item.category) tags.push(translateCategory(item.category));
        if (item.isMusic && item.source === "youtube" && !item.category)
          tags.push("Música");
        if (item.contentType === "short") tags.push("Short");
        if (item.contentType === "live") tags.push("Live");
        if (item.playbackRate && item.playbackRate !== 1)
          tags.push(`${item.playbackRate}x`);
        if (item.mostlyMuted) tags.push("🔇");
        if (item.imported) tags.push("Takeout");
        const sub = `${item.artist} · ${timeStr}${tags.length ? " · " + tags.join(" · ") : ""}`;
        return buildTrackRow(
          item.isLoop ? "🔁" : "•",
          item.artwork,
          item.title,
          sub,
          item.seconds > 0 ? formatClock(item.seconds) : "—",
          { href: resolveVideoUrl(item) || undefined },
        );
      }),
    );
    return;
  }
  if (uiState.recentMode === "likes" || uiState.recentMode === "subs") {
    const type = uiState.recentMode === "likes" ? "like" : "subscribe";
    const items = getFilteredMeta(store.interactions, true)
      .filter((it) => it.type === type)
      .sort((a, b) => b.timestamp - a.timestamp)
      .slice(0, 30);
    dom.recentEmpty.hidden = items.length > 0;
    dom.recentList.replaceChildren(...items.map(renderInteractionRow));
    return;
  }
  const searches = getFilteredMeta(store.searches, true)
    .sort((a, b) => b.timestamp - a.timestamp)
    .slice(0, 30);
  dom.recentEmpty.hidden = searches.length > 0;
  dom.recentList.replaceChildren(...searches.map(renderSearchRow));
}

function renderActivityTab() {
  const vm = activityVM(
    getFilteredPlays(true),
    getFilteredMeta(store.interactions, true),
    getFilteredMeta(store.searches, true),
    store.skips.filter((it) =>
      matchesFilter(it, dom.periodSelect.value, dom.sourceSelect.value, true),
    ),
  );

  dom.categoriesBox.hidden = vm.catEntries.length === 0;
  dom.categoriesList.replaceChildren(
    ...vm.catEntries.map((st) => {
      const row = document.createElement("div");
      row.className = "cat-row";
      const top = document.createElement("div");
      top.className = "cat-row-top";
      const lbl = document.createElement("span");
      lbl.textContent = translateCategory(st.cat);
      const meta = document.createElement("span");
      meta.textContent = `${st.pct}% · ${st.plays}x (${formatDuration(st.seconds)})`;
      top.append(lbl, meta);
      const track = document.createElement("div");
      track.className = "cat-track";
      const fill = document.createElement("div");
      fill.className = "cat-fill";
      fill.style.width = `${st.pct}%`;
      track.append(fill);
      row.append(top, track);
      return row;
    }),
  );

  dom.hoursChart.replaceChildren(
    ...vm.hours.map((sec, h) => {
      const bar = document.createElement("div");
      bar.className = sec === 0 ? "hour-bar zero" : "hour-bar";
      const pct =
        vm.maxHour > 0 ? Math.max(5, Math.round((sec / vm.maxHour) * 100)) : 5;
      bar.style.height = sec === 0 ? "4px" : `${pct}%`;
      bar.title = `${String(h).padStart(2, "0")}:00 — ${formatDuration(sec)}`;
      return bar;
    }),
  );

  dom.breakdownStats.replaceChildren(
    ...vm.chips.map(([label, val]) => {
      const div = document.createElement("div");
      div.className = "bd-chip";
      const sp = document.createElement("span");
      sp.textContent = label;
      const st = document.createElement("strong");
      st.textContent = String(val);
      div.append(sp, st);
      return div;
    }),
  );

  dom.searchesBox.hidden = vm.topSearches.length === 0;
  dom.topSearchesList.replaceChildren(
    ...vm.topSearches.map((s, idx) =>
      buildTrackRow(
        String(idx + 1),
        "",
        s.query,
        s.source === "yt_music" ? "YT Music" : "YouTube",
        `${s.count}x`,
        { href: s.url || undefined, noThumb: true },
      ),
    ),
  );

  dom.albumsBox.hidden = vm.topAlbums.length === 0;
  dom.topAlbumsList.replaceChildren(
    ...vm.topAlbums.map((a, idx) =>
      buildTrackRow(
        String(idx + 1),
        a.artwork,
        a.album,
        a.artist,
        `${a.plays}x · ${formatDuration(a.seconds)}`,
      ),
    ),
  );

  dom.topArtistsList.replaceChildren(
    ...vm.topArtists.map((st, idx) =>
      buildTrackRow(
        String(idx + 1),
        st.artwork,
        st.name,
        `${st.plays} reproducciones`,
        formatDuration(st.seconds),
        { href: st.url || undefined },
      ),
    ),
  );

  dom.skipsBox.hidden = vm.topSkips.length === 0;
  dom.topSkipsList.replaceChildren(
    ...vm.topSkips.map((s, idx) =>
      buildTrackRow(
        String(idx + 1),
        s.artwork,
        s.title,
        s.artist,
        `${s.count} skips`,
        { href: s.videoUrl || undefined },
      ),
    ),
  );
}

function renderCalendarTab() {
  renderCalendarView({
    dom,
    uiState,
    getFilteredPlays,
    getFilteredMeta,
    store,
    buildTrackRow,
    renderInteractionRow,
    renderSearchRow,
    onExportPng: (cardData, btn) => exportPngCard(cardData, btn),
  });
}

function renderChannelsTab() {
  const vm = channelsVM(
    getFilteredMeta(store.channelVisits, true),
    getFilteredPlays(true),
    uiState.channelSort,
    uiState.channelMinSec,
  );
  const newSubsCount = getFilteredMeta(store.interactions, true).filter(
    (it) => it.type === "subscribe",
  ).length;
  dom.chUniqueCount.textContent = String(vm.unique);
  dom.chTotalVisits.textContent = String(vm.totalVisits);
  dom.chNewSubs.textContent = `🔔 ${newSubsCount}`;

  dom.channelsDurationEmpty.hidden = vm.withTime.length > 0;
  dom.channelsDurationList.replaceChildren(
    ...vm.withTime.map((ch, idx) =>
      buildTrackRow(
        String(idx + 1),
        ch.avatar,
        ch.name,
        `${ch.plays} rep. · ${ch.sharePct}% del tiempo`,
        formatDuration(ch.seconds),
        { href: ch.url || undefined, avatar: true, barPct: ch.barPct },
      ),
    ),
  );

  dom.channelsEmpty.hidden = vm.ranked.length > 0;
  dom.channelsTopList.replaceChildren(
    ...vm.ranked.map((ch, idx) =>
      buildTrackRow(
        String(idx + 1),
        ch.avatar,
        ch.name,
        ch.sub,
        ch.seconds > 0 ? formatDuration(ch.seconds) : `${ch.visits}x`,
        { href: ch.url || undefined, avatar: true, barPct: ch.barPct },
      ),
    ),
  );

  dom.channelsRecentEmpty.hidden = vm.recent.length > 0;
  dom.channelsRecentList.replaceChildren(
    ...vm.recent.map((v) =>
      buildTrackRow(
        "👁",
        v.avatar,
        v.name,
        `${v.source === "yt_music" ? "YT Music" : "YouTube"} · ${formatShortDateTime(v.timestamp)}`,
        "Abrir ↗",
        { href: v.url || undefined, avatar: true },
      ),
    ),
  );
}

function renderLikesTab() {
  const vm = likesVM(
    getFilteredMeta(store.interactions, true),
    getFilteredMeta(store.searches, true),
  );
  dom.lkTotalLikes.textContent = `❤️ ${vm.likes.length}`;
  dom.lkTotalSubs.textContent = `🔔 ${vm.subs.length}`;
  dom.lkTotalSearches.textContent = `🔍 ${vm.searches.length}`;
  dom.likesEmpty.hidden = vm.likes.length > 0;
  dom.likesList.replaceChildren(
    ...vm.likes.slice(0, 30).map(renderInteractionRow),
  );
  dom.subsEmpty.hidden = vm.subs.length > 0;
  dom.subsList.replaceChildren(
    ...vm.subs.slice(0, 30).map(renderInteractionRow),
  );
  dom.socialSearchesEmpty.hidden = vm.searches.length > 0;
  dom.socialSearchesList.replaceChildren(
    ...vm.searches.slice(0, 25).map(renderSearchRow),
  );
}

const TAB_RENDERERS = {
  summary: renderSummaryTab,
  recent: renderRecentTab,
  activity: renderActivityTab,
  calendar: renderCalendarTab,
  channels: renderChannelsTab,
  likes: renderLikesTab,
};

function renderActiveTab() {
  TAB_RENDERERS[uiState.activeTab]?.();
  uiState.dirtyTabs.delete(uiState.activeTab);
}
function invalidateAllTabs() {
  Object.keys(TAB_RENDERERS).forEach((t) => uiState.dirtyTabs.add(t));
  renderActiveTab();
}

dom.exportBtn.addEventListener("click", async () => {
  const orig = dom.exportBtn.textContent;
  dom.exportBtn.disabled = true;
  dom.exportBtn.textContent = "⏳...";
  try {
    if (uiState.dirtyTabs.has("summary")) renderSummaryTab();
    const blob = await buildExportZip(
      store,
      uiState.summaryCardData,
      (p) => (dom.exportBtn.textContent = p),
    );
    downloadBlob(blob, `youtube-recap-${toDateKey(new Date())}.zip`);
    showStatus("✓ ZIP exportado con datos, tarjeta PNG e imágenes.");
  } catch (err) {
    showStatus(`Error al exportar: ${err.message}`, true);
  } finally {
    dom.exportBtn.disabled = false;
    dom.exportBtn.textContent = orig;
  }
});

  window.open(chrome.runtime.getURL("full.html?import=1"), "_blank");
});

dom.importInput.addEventListener("change", async (e) => {
  const file = e.target.files?.[0];
  if (!file) return;
  try {
    const { updatedData, message } = await importFromFile(file, store);
    await chrome.storage.local.set(updatedData);
    Object.assign(store, updatedData);
    if (migrateMusicFlags(store)) await persistMusicFlags(store);
    invalidateAllTabs();
    showStatus(message);
  } catch (err) {
    showStatus(`Error al importar: ${err.message}`, true);
  } finally {
    dom.importInput.value = "";
  }
});

dom.enrichMusicBtn.addEventListener("click", async () => {
  const orig = dom.enrichMusicBtn.textContent;
  dom.enrichMusicBtn.disabled = true;
  try {
    const res = await enrichImported(store, (p) => {
      dom.enrichMusicBtn.textContent = p;
    });
    if (res.none) {
      showStatus(
        "No hay registros de YouTube sin categoría para reclasificar.",
      );
      return;
    }
    if (res.updated > 0) {
      await persistMusicFlags(store);
      invalidateAllTabs();
    }
    showStatus(
      res.updated > 0
        ? `✓ Reclasificados ${res.updated} registros (${res.consulted} videos consultados).${res.remaining > 0 ? ` Quedan ${res.remaining} para otra corrida.` : ""}`
        : `Sin cambios: ${res.consulted} videos consultados, ninguno musical.${res.remaining > 0 ? ` Quedan ${res.remaining} para otra corrida.` : ""}`,
    );
  } catch (err) {
    showStatus(`Error al reclasificar: ${err.message}`, true);
  } finally {
    dom.enrichMusicBtn.disabled = false;
    dom.enrichMusicBtn.textContent = orig;
  }
});

chrome.tabs.onRemoved.addListener((tabId) => {
  npService.removeTab(tabId);
  renderBestNowPlaying();
});

// PATCH C (opcional): debounce 600ms y skip de re-render si el popup está oculto.
let dataRenderTimer = null;
chrome.storage.onChanged.addListener(async (changes, area) => {
  if (area !== "local") return;
  const { npChanged, needSync, dataChanged, updates } =
    npService.applyChanges(changes);
  if (needSync) await npService.sync();
  if (npChanged) renderBestNowPlaying();
  if (dataChanged) {
    Object.assign(store, updates);
    if (dataRenderTimer) clearTimeout(dataRenderTimer);
    dataRenderTimer = setTimeout(() => {
      if (!document.hidden) invalidateAllTabs();
    }, 600);
  }
});

async function init() {
  const [{ store: loaded, all }] = await Promise.all([
    loadStore(),
    npService.sync(),
  ]);
  Object.assign(store, loaded);
  if (!all.musicFlagMigratedV1) {
    const changed = migrateMusicFlags(store);
    const payload = { musicFlagMigratedV1: true };
    if (changed) {
      payload.history = store.history;
      payload.skips = store.skips;
      payload.interactions = store.interactions;
    }
    await chrome.storage.local.set(payload);
  }
  const staleKeys = npService.loadInitial(all);
  if (staleKeys.length > 0) chrome.storage.local.remove(staleKeys);
  renderBestNowPlaying();
  invalidateAllTabs();
  setInterval(renderBestNowPlaying, 3000);
}

init();

// ARCHIVO: C:\Users\christian\Desktop\Project\extensions\yt-recap-extension\src\recapStore.js

// src/recapStore.js

import {
  computeIsMusicFlag,
  sanitizeFileSlug,
  getExtFromMime,
  toDateKey,
} from "./utils.js";
import { buildZipBlob, extractJsonFromZipBuffer } from "./zip.js";
import { importBackupPayload } from "./takeout.js";
import { renderRecapPngBlob } from "./cardRenderer.js";

export const STORE_KEYS = [
  "history",
  "channelVisits",
  "skips",
  "interactions",
  "searches",
];

export function emptyStore() {
  return {
    history: [],
    channelVisits: [],
    skips: [],
    interactions: [],
    searches: [],
  };
}

export async function loadStore() {
  const all = await chrome.storage.local.get(null);
  const store = emptyStore();
  for (const key of STORE_KEYS) {
    store[key] = Array.isArray(all[key]) ? all[key] : [];
  }
  return { store, all };
}

export function migrateMusicFlags(data) {
  let changed = false;
  const byVideo = new Map();
  const byTrack = new Map();

  for (const it of data.history) {
    const flag =
      it.source === "yt_music" ||
      it.isMusic === true ||
      computeIsMusicFlag(it.category, "", it.artist);
    if (flag && it.isMusic !== true) {
      it.isMusic = true;
      changed = true;
    }
    if (it.isMusic) {
      if (it.videoId) byVideo.set(it.videoId, true);
      if (it.trackKey) byTrack.set(it.trackKey, true);
    }
  }
  for (const sk of data.skips) {
    const flag =
      sk.isMusic === true ||
      computeIsMusicFlag(sk.category, "", sk.artist) ||
      (sk.videoId && byVideo.get(sk.videoId)) ||
      (sk.trackKey && byTrack.get(sk.trackKey));
    if (flag && sk.isMusic !== true) {
      sk.isMusic = true;
      changed = true;
    }
  }
  for (const it of data.interactions) {
    if (it.type !== "like") continue;
    const flag =
      it.isMusic === true ||
      computeIsMusicFlag(it.category, "", it.artist) ||
      (it.videoId && byVideo.get(it.videoId)) ||
      (it.targetKey && byTrack.get(it.targetKey));
    if (flag && it.isMusic !== true) {
      it.isMusic = true;
      changed = true;
    }
  }
  return changed;
}

export async function persistMusicFlags(store) {
  await chrome.storage.local.set({
    history: store.history,
    skips: store.skips,
    interactions: store.interactions,
  });
}

export function downloadBlob(blob, name) {
  const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  const a = document.createElement("a");
  a.href = url;
  a.download = name;
  a.click();
  URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
}

export async function exportPngCard(cardData, btn) {
  if (!cardData || cardData.topTracks.length === 0) return;
  const orig = btn.textContent;
  btn.disabled = true;
  btn.textContent = "⏳...";
  try {
    const blob = await renderRecapPngBlob(cardData);
    if (!blob) return;
    downloadBlob(
      blob,
      `recap-${sanitizeFileSlug(cardData.subtitle)}-${toDateKey(new Date())}.png`,
    );
  } finally {
    btn.disabled = false;
    btn.textContent = orig;
  }
}

export async function buildExportZip(store, cardData, onProgress) {
  const imageTasks = [];
  const seenUrls = new Set();
  const usedNames = new Set();

  const queueImage = (url, folder, label) => {
    if (!url?.startsWith("http") || seenUrls.has(url)) return;
    seenUrls.add(url);
    const slug = sanitizeFileSlug(label);
    let unique = slug;
    let n = 2;
    while (usedNames.has(`${folder}/${unique}`)) unique = `${slug}-${n++}`;
    usedNames.add(`${folder}/${unique}`);
    imageTasks.push({ url, folder, slug: unique });
  };

  store.history.forEach((it) =>
    queueImage(it.artwork, "imagenes/portadas", it.videoId || it.title),
  );
  store.channelVisits.forEach((ch) =>
    queueImage(ch.avatar, "imagenes/canales", ch.name || ch.channelKey),
  );
  store.interactions.forEach((it) =>
    queueImage(
      it.artwork,
      it.type === "subscribe" ? "imagenes/canales" : "imagenes/portadas",
      it.title,
    ),
  );
  store.skips.forEach((sk) =>
    queueImage(sk.artwork, "imagenes/portadas", sk.videoId || sk.title),
  );

  const zipFiles = [];
  const imageMap = {};
  let done = 0;

  for (let i = 0; i < imageTasks.length; i += 6) {
    await Promise.all(
      imageTasks.slice(i, i + 6).map(async (task) => {
        try {
          const res = await fetch(task.url);
          if (!res.ok) return;
          const blob = await res.blob();
          const buf = new Uint8Array(await blob.arrayBuffer());
          const filePath = `${task.folder}/${task.slug}.${getExtFromMime(blob.type)}`;
          zipFiles.push({ name: filePath, data: buf });
          imageMap[task.url] = filePath;
        } catch {
          // Ignorar
        } finally {
          done += 1;
          onProgress?.(`⏳ ${done}/${imageTasks.length}`);
        }
      }),
    );
  }

  if (cardData) {
    const cardBlob = await renderRecapPngBlob(cardData);
    if (cardBlob) {
      zipFiles.push({
        name: "recap-tarjeta.png",
        data: new Uint8Array(await cardBlob.arrayBuffer()),
      });
    }
  }

  const payload = { exportedAt: new Date().toISOString(), ...store, imageMap };
  zipFiles.unshift({
    name: "recap-backup.json",
    data: new TextEncoder().encode(JSON.stringify(payload, null, 2)),
  });
  return buildZipBlob(zipFiles);
}

export async function importFromFile(file, store) {
  const isZip =
    file.name.toLowerCase().endsWith(".zip") || file.type.includes("zip");
  const parsed = isZip
    ? await extractJsonFromZipBuffer(await file.arrayBuffer())
    : JSON.parse(await file.text());
  return importBackupPayload(parsed, store);
}

const ENRICH_CONCURRENCY = 4;
const ENRICH_MAX_PER_RUN = 200;

export async function enrichImported(store, onProgress) {
  const candidates = [];
  const seen = new Set();
  const push = (it) => {
    if (
      it &&
      it.source === "youtube" &&
      !it.category &&
      !it.isMusic &&
      it.videoId &&
      !seen.has(it.videoId)
    ) {
      seen.add(it.videoId);
      candidates.push(it.videoId);
    }
  };
  store.history.forEach(push);
  store.skips.forEach(push);
  store.interactions.forEach((it) => it.type === "like" && push(it));

  if (candidates.length === 0) {
    return { updated: 0, consulted: 0, remaining: 0, none: true };
  }

  const batch = candidates.slice(0, ENRICH_MAX_PER_RUN);
  const remaining = candidates.length - batch.length;
  const results = new Map();
  let done = 0;

  for (let i = 0; i < batch.length; i += ENRICH_CONCURRENCY) {
    await Promise.all(
      batch.slice(i, i + ENRICH_CONCURRENCY).map(async (vid) => {
        try {
          const res = await fetch(
            `https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=${encodeURIComponent(vid)}`,
            { credentials: "omit" },
          );
          const html = res.ok ? await res.text() : "";
          const cat =
            html
              .match(/"category"\s*:\s*"([^"]+)"/)?.[1]
              ?.replace(/\\u0026/g, "&") || "";
          const mtype =
            html.match(/"musicVideoType"\s*:\s*"([^"]+)"/)?.[1] || "";
          results.set(vid, { cat, mtype });
        } catch {
          results.set(vid, { cat: "", mtype: "" });
        } finally {
          done += 1;
          onProgress?.(`⏳ ${done}/${batch.length}`);
        }
      }),
    );
  }

  let updated = 0;
  const apply = (it) => {
    if (!it || it.source !== "youtube" || !it.videoId) return;
    const r = results.get(it.videoId);
    if (!r || (!r.cat && !r.mtype)) return;
    let changed = false;
    if (r.cat && !it.category) {
      it.category = r.cat;
      changed = true;
    }
    if (
      computeIsMusicFlag(it.category, r.mtype, it.artist) &&
      it.isMusic !== true
    ) {
      it.isMusic = true;
      changed = true;
    }
    if (changed) updated += 1;
  };
  store.history.forEach(apply);
  store.skips.forEach(apply);
  store.interactions.forEach((it) => it.type === "like" && apply(it));

  return { updated, consulted: batch.length, remaining, none: false };
}

export class NowPlaying {
  constructor() {
    this.np = new Map();
    this.tabs = new Map();
  }

  async sync() {
    try {
      const allTabs = await chrome.tabs.query({});
      this.tabs.clear();
      for (const t of allTabs) {
        const u = t.url || t.pendingUrl || "";
        if (
          u.startsWith("https://www.youtube.com/") ||
          u.startsWith("https://music.youtube.com/")
        ) {
          this.tabs.set(t.id, t);
        }
      }
    } catch {
      // Ignorar si no hay permiso tabs
    }
  }

  loadInitial(all) {
    this.np.clear();
    const staleKeys = [];
    const now = Date.now();
    for (const [key, val] of Object.entries(all)) {
      if (!key.startsWith("np_") || !val) continue;
      if (key.startsWith("np_fb_")) {
        if (now - (val.updatedAt || 0) < 15000) this.np.set(key, val);
        else staleKeys.push(key);
      } else {
        const idPart = Number(key.slice(3));
        if (!Number.isInteger(idPart) || !this.tabs.has(idPart)) {
          staleKeys.push(key);
        } else {
          this.np.set(key, val);
        }
      }
    }
    return staleKeys;
  }

  applyChanges(changes) {
    let npChanged = false;
    let needSync = false;
    let dataChanged = false;
    const updates = {};
    for (const [key, change] of Object.entries(changes)) {
      if (key.startsWith("np_")) {
        if (change.newValue) {
          this.np.set(key, change.newValue);
          if (
            change.newValue.tabId != null &&
            !this.tabs.has(change.newValue.tabId)
          ) {
            needSync = true;
          }
        } else {
          this.np.delete(key);
        }
        npChanged = true;
      } else if (STORE_KEYS.includes(key)) {
        updates[key] = change.newValue || [];
        dataChanged = true;
      }
    }
    return { npChanged, needSync, dataChanged, updates };
  }

  removeTab(tabId) {
    this.tabs.delete(tabId);
    this.np.delete(`np_${tabId}`);
  }

  select(pinnedId) {
    const now = Date.now();
    const candidates = [...this.np.values()].filter((v) => {
      if (v.tabId != null) {
        if (!this.tabs.has(v.tabId)) return false;
        return v.paused
          ? now - v.updatedAt < 43200000
          : now - v.updatedAt < 90000;
      }
      return now - v.updatedAt < 15000;
    });
    if (candidates.length === 0) return null;
    candidates.sort((a, b) => {
      if (a.paused !== b.paused) return a.paused ? 1 : -1;
      if (a.visible !== b.visible) return a.visible ? -1 : 1;
      return b.updatedAt - a.updatedAt;
    });
    const pinned =
      pinnedId != null
        ? candidates.find((c) => (c.storageKey || c.tabId) === pinnedId)
        : null;
    const primary = pinned || candidates[0];
    const primaryKey = primary.storageKey || primary.tabId;
    const others = candidates.filter(
      (c) => (c.storageKey || c.tabId) !== primaryKey,
    );
    return { primary, others };
  }
}

// ARCHIVO: C:\Users\christian\Desktop\Project\extensions\yt-recap-extension\src\relativeTime.js

// src/relativeTime.js

const TIME_FMT = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("es-AR", {
  hour: "2-digit",
  minute: "2-digit",
  hour12: false,
});
const DAY_FMT = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("es-AR", {
  day: "numeric",
  month: "short",
});
const FULL_FMT = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("es-AR", {
  day: "2-digit",
  month: "2-digit",
  hour: "2-digit",
  minute: "2-digit",
  hour12: false,
});

export function formatTimeEs(ts) {
  return TIME_FMT.format(new Date(ts));
}

export function formatFullEs(ts) {
  return FULL_FMT.format(new Date(ts));
}

export function relativeTime(ts) {
  const now = Date.now();
  const diff = Math.max(0, now - ts);
  if (diff < 60000) return "ahora";
  const mins = Math.floor(diff / 60000);
  if (mins < 60) return `hace ${mins} min`;
  const d = new Date(ts);
  const today = new Date(now);
  const sameDay =
    d.getFullYear() === today.getFullYear() &&
    d.getMonth() === today.getMonth() &&
    d.getDate() === today.getDate();
  if (sameDay) return `hace ${Math.floor(mins / 60)} h`;
  const yest = new Date(now - 86400000);
  const isYesterday =
    d.getFullYear() === yest.getFullYear() &&
    d.getMonth() === yest.getMonth() &&
    d.getDate() === yest.getDate();
  if (isYesterday) return `ayer ${TIME_FMT.format(d)}`;
  return `${DAY_FMT.format(d)} ${TIME_FMT.format(d)}`;
}

export function greeting() {
  const h = new Date().getHours();
  if (h < 6) return "Buenas noches";
  if (h < 13) return "Buenos días";
  if (h < 20) return "Buenas tardes";
  return "Buenas noches";
}

// ARCHIVO: C:\Users\christian\Desktop\Project\extensions\yt-recap-extension\src\rows.js
// ==========================================

// src/rows.js

import { translateCategory, formatShortDateTime } from "./utils.js";

export function buildTrackRow(
  rankText,
  artwork,
  titleText,
  subText,
  rightText,
  opts = {},
) {
  const li = document.createElement("li");
  li.className = opts.href ? "top-item clickable" : "top-item";

  const rank = document.createElement("span");
  rank.className = "rank";
  rank.textContent = rankText;

  let img = null;
  if (!opts.noThumb) {
    img = document.createElement("img");
    img.className = opts.avatar ? "thumb avatar" : "thumb";
    if (artwork) img.src = artwork;
    img.alt = "";
    img.loading = "lazy";
    img.decoding = "async";
  }

  const meta = document.createElement("div");
  meta.className = "meta";

  const title = document.createElement("div");
  title.className = "track-title";
  title.textContent = titleText;

  const sub = document.createElement("div");
  sub.className = "track-sub";
  sub.textContent = subText;

  meta.append(title, sub);

  if (opts.barPct > 0) {
    const track = document.createElement("div");
    track.className = "ch-bar-track";
    const fill = document.createElement("div");
    fill.className = "ch-bar-fill";
    fill.style.width = `${Math.min(100, Math.max(3, opts.barPct))}%`;
    track.append(fill);
    meta.append(track);
  }

  const stats = document.createElement("div");
  stats.className = "track-stats";
  stats.textContent = rightText;

  const children = img ? [rank, img, meta, stats] : [rank, meta, stats];

  if (opts.href) {
    const a = document.createElement("a");
    a.className = "item-link";
    a.href = opts.href;
    a.target = "_blank";
    a.rel = "noopener noreferrer";
    a.append(...children);
    li.append(a);
  } else {
    li.append(...children);
  }
  return li;
}

export function renderInteractionRow(it) {
  const isLike = it.type === "like";
  const cat =
    isLike && it.category ? ` · ${translateCategory(it.category)}` : "";
  return buildTrackRow(
    isLike ? "❤️" : "🔔",
    it.artwork,
    it.title,
    `${isLike ? it.artist : "Suscripción nueva"} · ${formatShortDateTime(it.timestamp)}${cat}`,
    isLike ? "Abrir ↗" : "Canal ↗",
    {
      href: (isLike ? it.videoUrl : it.channelUrl) || undefined,
      avatar: !isLike,
    },
  );
}

export function renderSearchRow(s) {
  const src = s.source === "yt_music" ? "YT Music" : "YouTube";
  return buildTrackRow(
    "🔍",
    "",
    s.query,
    `${src} · ${formatShortDateTime(s.timestamp)}`,
    "Buscar ↗",
    { href: s.url || undefined, noThumb: true },
  );
}

// ==========================================

// src/takeout.js

function parseTakeoutWatchHistory(entries) {
  const imported = [];
  for (const item of entries) {
    if (!item?.titleUrl || !item?.time) continue;
    if (item.details?.some((d) => String(d.name || "").includes("Ads")))
      continue;

    let videoId = "";
    let contentType = "video";
    try {
      const u = new URL(item.titleUrl);
      videoId = u.searchParams.get("v") || "";
      if (!videoId) {
        const m = u.pathname.match(/^\/(?:shorts|live)\/([^/?#]+)/);
        if (m) videoId = m[1];
      }
      if (u.pathname.startsWith("/shorts/")) contentType = "short";
    } catch {
      continue;
    }

    if (!videoId) continue;

    const isYtMusic = item.header === "YouTube Music";
    if (isYtMusic && contentType === "video") contentType = "music";

    const cleanTitle = String(item.title || "")
      .replace(
        /^(Watched|Has visto|Viste|Miraste|Escuchaste|Listened to)\s+/i,
        "",
      )
      .trim();
    if (!cleanTitle) continue;

    const artist = item.subtitles?.[0]?.name?.trim() || "YouTube";
    const channelUrl = item.subtitles?.[0]?.url || "";
    const ts = new Date(item.time).getTime();
    if (!Number.isFinite(ts)) continue;

    imported.push({
      id: `takeout-${ts}-${videoId}`,
      trackKey: isYtMusic
        ? `ytm::${cleanTitle.toLowerCase()}::${artist.toLowerCase()}`
        : `yt::${videoId}::${cleanTitle.toLowerCase()}`,
      videoId,
      videoUrl: item.titleUrl,
      title: cleanTitle,
      artist,
      album: "",
      category: isYtMusic ? "Music" : "",
      artwork: `https://i.ytimg.com/vi/${videoId}/hqdefault.jpg`,
      channelUrl,
      source: isYtMusic ? "yt_music" : "youtube",
      contentType,
      context: "direct",
      playlistId: "",
      duration: 0,
      completionPct: null,
      playbackRate: 1,
      mostlyMuted: false,
      seconds: 0,
      isLoop: false,
      imported: true,
      timestamp: ts,
    });
  }
  return imported;
}

function parseTakeoutSearchHistory(entries) {
  const searches = [];
  for (const item of entries) {
    if (!item?.titleUrl || !item?.time) continue;
    if (
      !item.titleUrl.includes("search_query=") &&
      !item.titleUrl.includes("/search?")
    )
      continue;

    const query = String(item.title || "")
      .replace(/^(Searched for|Buscaste|Has buscado)\s+/i, "")
      .trim();
    const ts = new Date(item.time).getTime();
    if (!query || !Number.isFinite(ts)) continue;

    const isYtMusic = item.header === "YouTube Music";
    searches.push({
      id: `sr-takeout-${ts}-${query.toLowerCase().slice(0, 16)}`,
      query,
      normalized: query.toLowerCase(),
      url: item.titleUrl,
      source: isYtMusic ? "yt_music" : "youtube",
      timestamp: ts,
    });
  }
  return searches;
}

function mergeUniqueById(target, incoming) {
  if (!Array.isArray(incoming)) return 0;
  const ids = new Set(target.map((x) => x.id));
  let added = 0;
  for (const item of incoming) {
    if (item?.id && !ids.has(item.id)) {
      target.push(item);
      ids.add(item.id);
      added += 1;
    }
  }
  return added;
}

export function importBackupPayload(parsed, currentData) {
  const next = {
    history: [...currentData.history],
    channelVisits: [...currentData.channelVisits],
    skips: [...currentData.skips],
    interactions: [...currentData.interactions],
    searches: [...currentData.searches],
  };

  if (Array.isArray(parsed)) {
    const watchAdded = mergeUniqueById(
      next.history,
      parseTakeoutWatchHistory(parsed),
    );
    const searchAdded = mergeUniqueById(
      next.searches,
      parseTakeoutSearchHistory(parsed),
    );
    const parts = [];
    if (watchAdded > 0) parts.push(`${watchAdded} reproducciones`);
    if (searchAdded > 0) parts.push(`${searchAdded} búsquedas`);
    return {
      updatedData: next,
      message: parts.length
        ? `✓ Google Takeout importado: ${parts.join(" y ")}.`
        : "✓ Archivo analizado: no había registros nuevos para agregar.",
    };
  }

  if (parsed && Array.isArray(parsed.history)) {
    const addedPlays = mergeUniqueById(next.history, parsed.history);
    mergeUniqueById(next.channelVisits, parsed.channelVisits);
    mergeUniqueById(next.skips, parsed.skips);
    mergeUniqueById(next.interactions, parsed.interactions);
    mergeUniqueById(next.searches, parsed.searches);

    return {
      updatedData: next,
      message: `✓ Backup restaurado (+${addedPlays} reproducciones).`,
    };
  }

  throw new Error(
    "El archivo no tiene formato de backup de la extensión ni de Google Takeout.",
  );
}

// src/utils.js

export const CATEGORY_LABELS = {
  Music: "Música",
  Gaming: "Videojuegos",
  Entertainment: "Entretenimiento",
  "Science & Technology": "Ciencia y Tech",
  Education: "Educación",
  "People & Blogs": "Personas y Blogs",
  Comedy: "Comedia",
  "News & Politics": "Noticias",
  Sports: "Deportes",
  "Film & Animation": "Cine y Animación",
  "Howto & Style": "Tutoriales",
  "Autos & Vehicles": "Autos",
  "Pets & Animals": "Mascotas",
  "Travel & Events": "Viajes",
};

export function translateCategory(cat) {
  return cat ? CATEGORY_LABELS[cat] || cat : "";
}

// --- FASE 2: clasificación de contenido musical ---
// Canales auto-generados de artistas ("Artist - Topic" / "- Tema").
export function isTopicChannelName(name) {
  return /[-–—]\s*(topic|tema)\s*$/i.test(String(name || "").trim());
}

// Regla amplia (la misma que content.js): categoría Music (incluye
// AMVs/lyrics/covers) || musicVideoType presente || canal - Topic.
// Nota: content.js mantiene su propia copia porque es un content script
// clásico (no módulo). Si cambiás la regla, actualizá ambos lados.
export function computeIsMusicFlag(category, musicVideoType, artist) {
  return (
    category === "Music" ||
    Boolean(musicVideoType) ||
    isTopicChannelName(artist)
  );
}

// Punto de verdad único: música = plataforma YT Music o flag de contenido.
export function isMusicItem(item) {
  return (
    item?.source === "yt_music" ||
    item?.isMusic === true ||
    item?.contentType === "music"
  );
}

export function toDateKey(date) {
  const y = date.getFullYear();
  const m = String(date.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0");
  const d = String(date.getDate()).padStart(2, "0");
  return `${y}-${m}-${d}`;
}

export function extractIdFromItem(item) {
  if (item.videoId) return item.videoId;
  if (item.videoUrl) {
    try {
      const u = new URL(item.videoUrl);
      const v = u.searchParams.get("v");
      if (v) return v;
      const m = u.pathname.match(/^\/(?:shorts|live|embed)\/([^/?#]+)/);
      if (m) return m[1];
    } catch {
      return "";
    }
  }
  if (item.trackKey?.startsWith("yt::")) {
    const parts = item.trackKey.split("::");
    if (parts[1]?.length >= 8) return parts[1];
  }
  return "";
}

export function getGroupKey(item) {
  const normTitle = (item.title || "").trim().toLowerCase();
  if (item.source === "yt_music") {
    return `ytm::${normTitle}::${(item.artist || "").trim().toLowerCase()}`;
  }
  const vid = extractIdFromItem(item);
  return vid
    ? `yt::${vid}::${normTitle}`
    : `${item.source || "yt"}::${normTitle}`;
}

export function resolveVideoUrl(item) {
  if (item.videoUrl) return item.videoUrl;
  const vid = extractIdFromItem(item);
  if (!vid) return "";
  return item.source === "yt_music"
    ? `https://music.youtube.com/watch?v=${vid}`
    : `https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=${vid}`;
}

export function openInstantPlaylist(videoIds) {
  const uniqueIds = [...new Set(videoIds.filter(Boolean))].slice(0, 50);
  if (uniqueIds.length === 0) return;
  window.open(
    `https://www.youtube.com/watch_videos?video_ids=${uniqueIds.join(",")}`,
    "_blank",
  );
}

export function formatClock(secs) {
  const s = Math.max(0, Math.floor(secs));
  const mins = Math.floor(s / 60);
  const rem = s % 60;
  return `${mins}:${String(rem).padStart(2, "0")}`;
}

export function formatDuration(totalSeconds) {
  const mins = Math.round(totalSeconds / 60);
  if (mins < 60) return `${mins} min`;
  const hrs = Math.floor(mins / 60);
  const rem = mins % 60;
  return `${hrs}h ${rem}m`;
}

export function formatShortDateTime(ts) {
  const d = new Date(ts);
  const dateStr = d.toLocaleDateString([], {
    day: "2-digit",
    month: "2-digit",
  });
  const timeStr = d.toLocaleTimeString([], {
    hour: "2-digit",
    minute: "2-digit",
  });
  return `${dateStr} ${timeStr}`;
}

export function computeStreak(items) {
  if (items.length === 0) return 0;
  const daysSet = new Set(
    items.map((item) => toDateKey(new Date(item.timestamp))),
  );
  let streak = 0;
  const cursor = new Date();

  while (true) {
    const key = toDateKey(cursor);
    if (daysSet.has(key)) {
      streak += 1;
      cursor.setDate(cursor.getDate() - 1);
    } else if (streak === 0) {
      cursor.setDate(cursor.getDate() - 1);
      if (!daysSet.has(toDateKey(cursor))) break;
    } else {
      break;
    }
  }
  return streak;
}

export function matchesFilter(
  item,
  periodValue,
  sourceValue,
  checkDate = true,
) {
  if (checkDate && periodValue !== "all") {
    const cutoff = Date.now() - Number(periodValue) * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
    if (item.timestamp < cutoff) return false;
  }
  const cType =
    item.contentType || (item.source === "yt_music" ? "music" : "video");
  // FASE 2: "YT Music" = contenido musical (cualquier plataforma);
  // "YouTube" = plataforma YouTube sin contenido musical.
  if (sourceValue === "yt_music") return isMusicItem(item);
  if (sourceValue === "youtube")
    return item.source === "youtube" && !isMusicItem(item);
  if (sourceValue === "no_shorts") return cType !== "short";
  if (sourceValue === "short") return cType === "short";
  if (sourceValue === "live") return cType === "live";
  return true;
}

export function matchesPeriodAndSourceOnly(
  item,
  periodValue,
  sourceValue,
  checkDate = true,
) {
  if (checkDate && periodValue !== "all") {
    const cutoff = Date.now() - Number(periodValue) * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
    if (item.timestamp < cutoff) return false;
  }
  if (sourceValue === "yt_music") return isMusicItem(item);
  if (sourceValue === "youtube")
    return item.source === "youtube" && !isMusicItem(item);
  return true;
}

export function aggregateTracks(items) {
  const map = new Map();
  for (const item of items) {
    const key = getGroupKey(item);
    const current = map.get(key) || {
      title: item.title,
      artist: item.artist,
      artwork: item.artwork,
      videoId: extractIdFromItem(item),
      videoUrl: resolveVideoUrl(item),
      plays: 0,
      loops: 0,
      seconds: 0,
      pctSum: 0,
      pctCount: 0,
    };
    current.plays += 1;
    if (item.isLoop) current.loops += 1;
    current.seconds += item.seconds || 0;
    if (typeof item.completionPct === "number") {
      current.pctSum += item.completionPct;
      current.pctCount += 1;
    }
    if (!current.artwork && item.artwork) current.artwork = item.artwork;
    if (!current.videoId) current.videoId = extractIdFromItem(item);
    if (!current.videoUrl) current.videoUrl = resolveVideoUrl(item);
    map.set(key, current);
  }
  return [...map.values()].sort(
    (a, b) => b.plays - a.plays || b.seconds - a.seconds,
  );
}

export function sanitizeFileSlug(text) {
  return (
    String(text || "imagen")
      .normalize("NFD")
      .replace(/[̀-ͯ]/g, "")
      .toLowerCase()
      .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "-")
      .replace(/^-+|-+$/g, "")
      .slice(0, 48) || "imagen"
  );
}

export function getExtFromMime(mime = "") {
  if (mime.includes("png")) return "png";
  if (mime.includes("webp")) return "webp";
  return "jpg";
}

// ARCHIVO: C:\Users\christian\Desktop\Project\extensions\yt-recap-extension\src\viewModels.js

// src/viewModels.js

import {
  aggregateTracks,
  computeStreak,
  formatDuration,
  getGroupKey,
  resolveVideoUrl,
  extractIdFromItem,
  isMusicItem,
  toDateKey,
} from "./utils.js";

function esc(s) {
  return String(s ?? "").replace(
    /[&<>"']/g,
    (c) =>
      ({ "&": "&amp;", "<": "&lt;", ">": "&gt;", '"': "&quot;", "'": "&#39;" })[
        c
      ],
  );
}

export function summaryVM(filtered) {
  if (filtered.length === 0) return { empty: true };
  let totalSeconds = 0;
  let totalLoops = 0;
  const hoursBuckets = new Array(24).fill(0);
  const artistsMap = new Map();
  for (const item of filtered) {
    totalSeconds += item.seconds || 0;
    if (item.isLoop) totalLoops += 1;
    hoursBuckets[new Date(item.timestamp).getHours()] += item.seconds || 0;
    const ak = item.artist || "Desconocido";
    const cur = artistsMap.get(ak) || { plays: 0, seconds: 0 };
    cur.plays += 1;
    cur.seconds += item.seconds || 0;
    artistsMap.set(ak, cur);
  }
  const topTracks = aggregateTracks(filtered);
  const topArtist =
    [...artistsMap.entries()].sort(
      (a, b) => b[1].seconds - a[1].seconds || b[1].plays - a[1].plays,
    )[0]?.[0] || "—";
  const maxHourSec = Math.max(...hoursBuckets);
  const peakIdx = hoursBuckets.indexOf(maxHourSec);
  return {
    empty: false,
    totalSeconds,
    totalPlays: filtered.length,
    totalLoops,
    unique: topTracks.length,
    streak: computeStreak(filtered),
    peakLabel: maxHourSec > 0 ? `${String(peakIdx).padStart(2, "0")}:00h` : "—",
    topTracks,
    topArtist,
  };
}

export function activityVM(
  filtered,
  filteredInter,
  filteredSearches,
  filteredSkips,
) {
  const likesCount = filteredInter.filter((it) => it.type === "like").length;
  const subsCount = filteredInter.filter(
    (it) => it.type === "subscribe",
  ).length;
  const hours = new Array(24).fill(0);
  const formatCounts = { video: 0, music: 0, short: 0, live: 0 };
  const contextCounts = { direct: 0, mix: 0, playlist: 0 };
  const categoriesMap = new Map();
  const artistsMap = new Map();
  const albumsMap = new Map();
  let rateSum = 0;
  let rateCount = 0;

  for (const item of filtered) {
    hours[new Date(item.timestamp).getHours()] += item.seconds || 0;
    const cType =
      item.contentType || (item.source === "yt_music" ? "music" : "video");
    formatCounts[cType] = (formatCounts[cType] || 0) + 1;
    const ctx = item.context || "direct";
    contextCounts[ctx] = (contextCounts[ctx] || 0) + 1;
    const catKey = item.category || (isMusicItem(item) ? "Music" : "");
    if (catKey) {
      const c = categoriesMap.get(catKey) || { plays: 0, seconds: 0 };
      c.plays += 1;
      c.seconds += item.seconds || 0;
      categoriesMap.set(catKey, c);
    }
    if (item.playbackRate) {
      rateSum += item.playbackRate;
      rateCount += 1;
    }
    const ak = item.artist || "Desconocido";
    const as = artistsMap.get(ak) || {
      plays: 0,
      seconds: 0,
      artwork: item.artwork,
      url: item.channelUrl || "",
    };
    as.plays += 1;
    as.seconds += item.seconds || 0;
    if (!as.artwork && item.artwork) as.artwork = item.artwork;
    if (!as.url && item.channelUrl) as.url = item.channelUrl;
    artistsMap.set(ak, as);
    if (item.album) {
      const albKey = `${item.album.toLowerCase()}::${ak.toLowerCase()}`;
      const alb = albumsMap.get(albKey) || {
        album: item.album,
        artist: item.artist,
        artwork: item.artwork,
        plays: 0,
        seconds: 0,
      };
      alb.plays += 1;
      alb.seconds += item.seconds || 0;
      if (!alb.artwork && item.artwork) alb.artwork = item.artwork;
      albumsMap.set(albKey, alb);
    }
  }

  const catSorted = [...categoriesMap.entries()].sort(
    (a, b) => b[1].seconds - a[1].seconds || b[1].plays - a[1].plays,
  );
  const totalWeight = catSorted.reduce(
    (acc, [, st]) => acc + (st.seconds || st.plays),
    0,
  );
  const catEntries = catSorted.slice(0, 6).map(([cat, st]) => ({
    cat,
    plays: st.plays,
    seconds: st.seconds,
    pct:
      totalWeight > 0
        ? Math.max(
            4,
            Math.round(((st.seconds || st.plays) / totalWeight) * 100),
          )
        : 0,
  }));

  const avgRate =
    rateCount > 0
      ? `${(rateSum / rateCount).toFixed(2).replace(/\.00$/, "")}x`
      : "1x";
  const musicCount = filtered.filter(isMusicItem).length;
  const musicOnYouTube = filtered.filter(
    (it) => it.source === "youtube" && isMusicItem(it),
  ).length;
  const videosNonMusic = filtered.filter((it) => {
    const cType =
      it.contentType || (it.source === "yt_music" ? "music" : "video");
    return cType === "video" && !isMusicItem(it);
  }).length;

  const chips = [
    ["Música", musicCount],
    ["Música en YouTube", musicOnYouTube],
    ["Videos (no música)", videosNonMusic],
    ["Shorts", formatCounts.short || 0],
    ["Directos", formatCounts.live || 0],
    [
      "Mix / Playlist",
      (contextCounts.mix || 0) + (contextCounts.playlist || 0),
    ],
    ["Vel. promedio", avgRate],
    ["❤️ Me gusta", likesCount],
    ["🔍 Búsquedas", filteredSearches.length],
    ["🔔 Suscrip.", subsCount],
  ];

  const sMap = new Map();
  for (const s of filteredSearches) {
    const cur = sMap.get(s.normalized) || {
      query: s.query,
      url: s.url,
      source: s.source,
      count: 0,
    };
    cur.count += 1;
    if (s.url) cur.url = s.url;
    sMap.set(s.normalized, cur);
  }
  const topSearches = [...sMap.values()]
    .sort((a, b) => b.count - a.count)
    .slice(0, 8);
  const topAlbums = [...albumsMap.values()]
    .sort((a, b) => b.plays - a.plays || b.seconds - a.seconds)
    .slice(0, 5);
  const topArtists = [...artistsMap.entries()]
    .sort((a, b) => b[1].seconds - a[1].seconds || b[1].plays - a[1].plays)
    .slice(0, 5)
    .map(([name, st]) => ({ name, ...st }));

  const skipsMap = new Map();
  for (const sk of filteredSkips) {
    const key = getGroupKey(sk);
    const cur = skipsMap.get(key) || {
      title: sk.title,
      artist: sk.artist,
      artwork: sk.artwork,
      videoUrl: resolveVideoUrl(sk),
      count: 0,
    };
    cur.count += 1;
    skipsMap.set(key, cur);
  }
  const topSkips = [...skipsMap.values()]
    .sort((a, b) => b.count - a.count)
    .slice(0, 5);

  return {
    hours,
    maxHour: Math.max(...hours),
    catEntries,
    chips,
    topSearches,
    topAlbums,
    topArtists,
    topSkips,
    likesCount,
    subsCount,
    searchesCount: filteredSearches.length,
    avgRate,
    mixCount: (contextCounts.mix || 0) + (contextCounts.playlist || 0),
  };
}

export function channelsVM(filteredVisits, filteredPlays, sort, minSec) {
  const channelsMap = new Map();
  const getCh = (key, name) => {
    if (!channelsMap.has(key))
      channelsMap.set(key, {
        name: name || "Canal",
        avatar: "",
        url: "",
        visits: 0,
        plays: 0,
        seconds: 0,
      });
    return channelsMap.get(key);
  };
  for (const v of filteredVisits) {
    const ch = getCh(
      (v.name || v.channelKey || "canal").trim().toLowerCase(),
      v.name,
    );
    ch.visits += 1;
    if (v.avatar) ch.avatar = v.avatar;
    if (v.url) ch.url = v.url;
  }
  for (const p of filteredPlays) {
    const ch = getCh((p.artist || "youtube").trim().toLowerCase(), p.artist);
    ch.plays += 1;
    ch.seconds += p.seconds || 0;
    if (!ch.url && p.channelUrl) ch.url = p.channelUrl;
    if (!ch.avatar && p.artwork) ch.avatar = p.artwork;
  }
  const allChannels = [...channelsMap.values()];
  const withTimeRaw = allChannels
    .filter((c) => c.seconds > 0)
    .sort((a, b) => b.seconds - a.seconds || b.plays - a.plays);
  const totalChannelsSec = withTimeRaw.reduce((acc, c) => acc + c.seconds, 0);
  const maxTimeSec = withTimeRaw[0]?.seconds || 0;
  const withTime = withTimeRaw.slice(0, 15).map((ch) => ({
    ...ch,
    sharePct:
      totalChannelsSec > 0
        ? Math.round((ch.seconds / totalChannelsSec) * 100)
        : 0,
    barPct: maxTimeSec > 0 ? Math.round((ch.seconds / maxTimeSec) * 100) : 0,
  }));
  const filtered = allChannels.filter((ch) => ch.seconds >= minSec);
  filtered.sort((a, b) => {
    if (sort === "time")
      return b.seconds - a.seconds || b.plays - a.plays || b.visits - a.visits;
    if (sort === "plays")
      return b.plays - a.plays || b.seconds - a.seconds || b.visits - a.visits;
    return b.visits - a.visits || b.seconds - a.seconds || b.plays - a.plays;
  });
  const maxRankSec = Math.max(0, ...filtered.map((c) => c.seconds));
  const ranked = filtered.slice(0, 25).map((ch) => {
    const subParts = [];
    if (ch.visits > 0)
      subParts.push(
        `👁️ ${ch.visits} ${ch.visits === 1 ? "visita" : "visitas"}`,
      );
    if (ch.plays > 0) subParts.push(`▶ ${ch.plays} rep.`);
    if (subParts.length === 0) subParts.push("Sin datos");
    return {
      ...ch,
      sub: subParts.join(" · "),
      barPct:
        maxRankSec > 0 && ch.seconds > 0
          ? Math.round((ch.seconds / maxRankSec) * 100)
          : 0,
    };
  });
  const recent = [...filteredVisits]
    .sort((a, b) => b.timestamp - a.timestamp)
    .slice(0, 20);
  return {
    unique: allChannels.length,
    totalVisits: filteredVisits.length,
    withTime,
    ranked,
    recent,
    allChannels,
  };
}

export function likesVM(filteredInter, filteredSearches) {
  return {
    likes: filteredInter
      .filter((it) => it.type === "like")
      .sort((a, b) => b.timestamp - a.timestamp),
    subs: filteredInter
      .filter((it) => it.type === "subscribe")
      .sort((a, b) => b.timestamp - a.timestamp),
    searches: [...filteredSearches].sort((a, b) => b.timestamp - a.timestamp),
  };
}

export function homeVM(periodPlays, allPlays, inter, searches, visits) {
  const top = aggregateTracks(periodPlays).slice(0, 12);
  const likedKeys = new Set();
  for (const it of inter) {
    if (it.type !== "like") continue;
    if (it.videoId) likedKeys.add(it.videoId);
    if (it.targetKey) likedKeys.add(it.targetKey);
  }
  const forgotMap = new Map();
  for (const p of allPlays) {
    const key = getGroupKey(p);
    const cur = forgotMap.get(key) || {
      title: p.title,
      artist: p.artist,
      artwork: p.artwork,
      videoUrl: resolveVideoUrl(p),
      videoId: extractIdFromItem(p),
      plays: 0,
      lastTs: 0,
      liked: false,
    };
    cur.plays += 1;
    cur.lastTs = Math.max(cur.lastTs, p.timestamp);
    if (likedKeys.has(p.videoId) || likedKeys.has(key)) cur.liked = true;
    if (!cur.artwork && p.artwork) cur.artwork = p.artwork;
    forgotMap.set(key, cur);
  }
  const cutoff = Date.now() - 30 * 86400000;
  const forgotten = [...forgotMap.values()]
    .filter((t) => (t.plays >= 2 || t.liked) && t.lastTs < cutoff)
    .sort((a, b) => b.plays - a.plays || b.lastTs - a.lastTs)
    .slice(0, 12);

  const artistsMap = new Map();
  const albumsMap = new Map();
  for (const p of periodPlays) {
    const ak = p.artist || "Desconocido";
    const a = artistsMap.get(ak) || {
      name: ak,
      plays: 0,
      seconds: 0,
      artwork: p.artwork,
      url: p.channelUrl || "",
    };
    a.plays += 1;
    a.seconds += p.seconds || 0;
    if (!a.artwork && p.artwork) a.artwork = p.artwork;
    if (!a.url && p.channelUrl) a.url = p.channelUrl;
    artistsMap.set(ak, a);
    if (p.album) {
      const k = `${p.album.toLowerCase()}::${ak.toLowerCase()}`;
      const al = albumsMap.get(k) || {
        album: p.album,
        artist: p.artist,
        artwork: p.artwork,
        plays: 0,
        seconds: 0,
      };
      al.plays += 1;
      al.seconds += p.seconds || 0;
      if (!al.artwork && p.artwork) al.artwork = p.artwork;
      albumsMap.set(k, al);
    }
  }
  const artists = [...artistsMap.values()]
    .sort((a, b) => b.seconds - a.seconds || b.plays - a.plays)
    .slice(0, 12);
  const albums = [...albumsMap.values()]
    .sort((a, b) => b.plays - a.plays || b.seconds - a.seconds)
    .slice(0, 12);

  const months = [];
  const now = new Date();
  for (let i = 0; i < 12; i++) {
    const d = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth() - i, 1);
    const y = d.getFullYear();
    const m = d.getMonth();
    let plays = 0;
    let seconds = 0;
    for (const p of allPlays) {
      const pd = new Date(p.timestamp);
      if (pd.getFullYear() === y && pd.getMonth() === m) {
        plays += 1;
        seconds += p.seconds || 0;
      }
    }
    months.push({
      y,
      m,
      label: new Intl.DateTimeFormat("es-AR", { month: "long" }).format(d),
      year: y,
      plays,
      seconds,
    });
  }

  const recent = [...periodPlays]
    .sort((a, b) => b.timestamp - a.timestamp)
    .slice(0, 12);
  const shorts = periodPlays
    .filter((p) => p.contentType === "short")
    .sort((a, b) => b.timestamp - a.timestamp)
    .slice(0, 12);
  const lives = periodPlays
    .filter((p) => p.contentType === "live")
    .sort((a, b) => b.timestamp - a.timestamp)
    .slice(0, 12);
  const recentSearches = [...searches]
    .sort((a, b) => b.timestamp - a.timestamp)
    .slice(0, 12);

  const chMap = new Map();
  for (const p of periodPlays) {
    const ak = p.artist || "YouTube";
    const c = chMap.get(ak) || {
      name: ak,
      plays: 0,
      seconds: 0,
      artwork: p.artwork,
      url: p.channelUrl || "",
    };
    c.plays += 1;
    c.seconds += p.seconds || 0;
    if (!c.artwork && p.artwork) c.artwork = p.artwork;
    if (!c.url && p.channelUrl) c.url = p.channelUrl;
    chMap.set(ak, c);
  }
  for (const v of visits) {
    const k = (v.name || v.channelKey || "canal").trim().toLowerCase();
    const c = chMap.get(k) || {
      name: v.name || "Canal",
      plays: 0,
      seconds: 0,
      artwork: v.avatar,
      url: v.url,
    };
    if (!c.artwork && v.avatar) c.artwork = v.avatar;
    if (!c.url && v.url) c.url = v.url;
    chMap.set(k, c);
  }
  const channels = [...chMap.values()]
    .sort((a, b) => b.seconds - a.seconds || b.plays - a.plays)
    .slice(0, 12);

  return {
    top,
    forgotten,
    artists,
    albums,
    months,
    recent,
    shorts,
    lives,
    recentSearches,
    channels,
  };
}

export function searchVM(query, scopeValue, store) {
  const q = query.trim().toLowerCase();
  if (!q) return null;
  const inScope = (it) =>
    scopeValue === "yt_music"
      ? isMusicItem(it)
      : it.source === "youtube" && !isMusicItem(it);
  const match = (it) =>
    (it.title || "").toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
    (it.artist || "").toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
    (it.album || "").toLowerCase().includes(q);
  const plays = store.history.filter((it) => inScope(it) && match(it));
  const tracks = aggregateTracks(plays).slice(0, 8);
  const artistsMap = new Map();
  for (const p of plays) {
    const ak = p.artist || "Desconocido";
    if (!ak.toLowerCase().includes(q)) continue;
    const a = artistsMap.get(ak) || {
      name: ak,
      plays: 0,
      seconds: 0,
      artwork: p.artwork,
      url: p.channelUrl || "",
    };
    a.plays += 1;
    a.seconds += p.seconds || 0;
    if (!a.artwork && p.artwork) a.artwork = p.artwork;
    if (!a.url && p.channelUrl) a.url = p.channelUrl;
    artistsMap.set(ak, a);
  }
  const artists = [...artistsMap.values()]
    .sort((a, b) => b.seconds - a.seconds)
    .slice(0, 6);
  const channels = store.channelVisits
    .filter(
      (v) =>
        (scopeValue === "yt_music"
          ? v.source === "yt_music"
          : v.source === "youtube") && (v.name || "").toLowerCase().includes(q),
    )
    .slice(0, 6);
  const searches = store.searches
    .filter(
      (s) =>
        (scopeValue === "yt_music"
          ? s.source === "yt_music"
          : s.source === "youtube") &&
        (s.query || "").toLowerCase().includes(q),
    )
    .slice(0, 6);
  return { tracks, artists, channels, searches };
}

/* ===================== v2.6 ===================== */

export function exploreInsightsVM(plays, prevPlays) {
  const sum = summaryVM(plays);
  const prev = summaryVM(prevPlays);
  const pct = (cur, old) =>
    old > 0 ? Math.round(((cur - old) / old) * 100) : null;
  const deltas =
    prev.empty || sum.empty
      ? null
      : {
          seconds: pct(sum.totalSeconds, prev.totalSeconds),
          plays: pct(sum.totalPlays, prev.totalPlays),
          unique: pct(sum.unique, prev.unique),
        };
  const days = 14;
  const trend = new Array(days).fill(0);
  const base = new Date();
  base.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
  const start = base.getTime() - (days - 1) * 86400000;
  for (const p of plays) {
    const d = new Date(p.timestamp);
    d.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
    const idx = Math.round((d.getTime() - start) / 86400000);
    if (idx >= 0 && idx < days) trend[idx] += p.seconds || 0;
  }
  return { sum, prev, deltas, trend, hasPrev: !prev.empty };
}

export function formatsVM(plays) {
  const c = { music: 0, video: 0, short: 0, live: 0 };
  for (const p of plays) {
    if (p.contentType === "short") c.short += 1;
    else if (p.contentType === "live") c.live += 1;
    else if (isMusicItem(p)) c.music += 1;
    else c.video += 1;
  }
  return [
    { key: "music", label: "Música", value: c.music, color: "var(--chart-1)" },
    { key: "video", label: "Videos", value: c.video, color: "var(--chart-2)" },
    { key: "short", label: "Shorts", value: c.short, color: "var(--chart-3)" },
    { key: "live", label: "Directos", value: c.live, color: "var(--chart-4)" },
  ].filter((s) => s.value > 0);
}

export function calendarHeroVM(plays, inter, searches, year) {
  let yearSeconds = 0;
  let yearPlays = 0;
  const monthSec = new Array(12).fill(0);
  const dayAny = new Set();
  for (const p of plays) {
    const d = new Date(p.timestamp);
    if (d.getFullYear() !== year) continue;
    yearSeconds += p.seconds || 0;
    yearPlays += 1;
    monthSec[d.getMonth()] += p.seconds || 0;
    dayAny.add(toDateKey(d));
  }
  for (const it of inter) {
    const d = new Date(it.timestamp);
    if (d.getFullYear() === year) dayAny.add(toDateKey(d));
  }
  for (const s of searches) {
    const d = new Date(s.timestamp);
    if (d.getFullYear() === year) dayAny.add(toDateKey(d));
  }
  const days = [...dayAny].sort();
  let maxStreak = 0,
    run = 0,
    prevT = null;
  for (const k of days) {
    const t = Date.parse(`${k}T00:00:00Z`);
    if (prevT != null && t - prevT === 86400000) run += 1;
    else run = 1;
    if (run > maxStreak) maxStreak = run;
    prevT = t;
  }
  const bestVal = Math.max(...monthSec);
  const bestIdx = monthSec.indexOf(bestVal);
  const bestMonth =
    bestVal > 0
      ? {
          label: new Intl.DateTimeFormat("es-AR", { month: "long" }).format(
            new Date(year, bestIdx, 1),
          ),
          seconds: bestVal,
          index: bestIdx,
        }
      : null;
  return {
    yearSeconds,
    yearPlays,
    activeDays: dayAny.size,
    maxStreak,
    bestMonth,
  };
}

export function artistDirectoryVM(plays, inter, sort = "time", query = "") {
  const map = new Map();
  for (const p of plays) {
    const name = p.artist || "Desconocido";
    const a = map.get(name) || {
      name,
      plays: 0,
      seconds: 0,
      artwork: "",
      url: "",
      likes: 0,
      albums: new Set(),
      lastTs: 0,
    };
    a.plays += 1;
    a.seconds += p.seconds || 0;
    a.lastTs = Math.max(a.lastTs, p.timestamp);
    if (!a.artwork && p.artwork) a.artwork = p.artwork;
    if (!a.url && p.channelUrl) a.url = p.channelUrl;
    if (p.album) a.albums.add(p.album);
    map.set(name, a);
  }
  for (const it of inter) {
    if (it.type !== "like") continue;
    const a = map.get(it.artist);
    if (a) a.likes += 1;
  }
  let list = [...map.values()].map((a) => ({ ...a, albums: a.albums.size }));
  const q = query.trim().toLowerCase();
  if (q) list = list.filter((a) => a.name.toLowerCase().includes(q));
  list.sort((a, b) => {
    if (sort === "plays") return b.plays - a.plays || b.seconds - a.seconds;
    if (sort === "name") return a.name.localeCompare(b.name, "es");
    return b.seconds - a.seconds || b.plays - a.plays;
  });
  return {
    list,
    totals: {
      count: list.length,
      seconds: list.reduce((s, a) => s + a.seconds, 0),
      plays: list.reduce((s, a) => s + a.plays, 0),
    },
  };
}

export function artistDetailVM(name, plays, inter) {
  const key = name.toLowerCase();
  const mine = plays.filter(
    (p) => (p.artist || "Desconocido").toLowerCase() === key,
  );
  const topTracks = aggregateTracks(mine).slice(0, 20);
  const albumsMap = new Map();
  const monthsMap = new Map();
  let seconds = 0,
    lastTs = 0,
    artwork = "",
    url = "";
  for (const p of mine) {
    seconds += p.seconds || 0;
    lastTs = Math.max(lastTs, p.timestamp);
    if (!artwork && p.artwork) artwork = p.artwork;
    if (!url && p.channelUrl) url = p.channelUrl;
    if (p.album) {
      const al = albumsMap.get(p.album) || {
        album: p.album,
        artist: p.artist,
        artwork: p.artwork,
        plays: 0,
        seconds: 0,
      };
      al.plays += 1;
      al.seconds += p.seconds || 0;
      if (!al.artwork && p.artwork) al.artwork = p.artwork;
      albumsMap.set(p.album, al);
    }
    const d = new Date(p.timestamp);
    const mk = `${d.getFullYear()}-${d.getMonth()}`;
    const mo = monthsMap.get(mk) || {
      y: d.getFullYear(),
      m: d.getMonth(),
      label: new Intl.DateTimeFormat("es-AR", { month: "long" }).format(d),
      year: d.getFullYear(),
      plays: 0,
      seconds: 0,
    };
    mo.plays += 1;
    mo.seconds += p.seconds || 0;
    monthsMap.set(mk, mo);
  }
  const likes = inter
    .filter(
      (it) => it.type === "like" && (it.artist || "").toLowerCase() === key,
    )
    .sort((a, b) => b.timestamp - a.timestamp);
  return {
    name: mine[0]?.artist || name,
    artwork,
    url,
    plays: mine.length,
    seconds,
    lastTs,
    topTracks,
    albums: [...albumsMap.values()].sort((a, b) => b.seconds - a.seconds),
    months: [...monthsMap.values()]
      .sort((a, b) => b.y - a.y || b.m - a.m)
      .slice(0, 12),
    likes,
    ids: topTracks.map((t) => t.videoId).filter(Boolean),
  };
}

export function channelDirectoryVM(
  plays,
  visits,
  sort = "time",
  minSec = 0,
  query = "",
) {
  const map = new Map();
  const get = (name) => {
    const k = (name || "Canal").trim().toLowerCase();
    if (!map.has(k))
      map.set(k, {
        name: name || "Canal",
        avatar: "",
        url: "",
        visits: 0,
        plays: 0,
        seconds: 0,
        lastTs: 0,
      });
    return map.get(k);
  };
  for (const p of plays) {
    const c = get(p.artist);
    c.plays += 1;
    c.seconds += p.seconds || 0;
    c.lastTs = Math.max(c.lastTs, p.timestamp);
    if (!c.avatar && p.artwork) c.avatar = p.artwork;
    if (!c.url && p.channelUrl) c.url = p.channelUrl;
  }
  for (const v of visits) {
    const c = get(v.name || v.channelKey);
    c.visits += 1;
    c.lastTs = Math.max(c.lastTs, v.timestamp);
    if (v.avatar) c.avatar = v.avatar;
    if (v.url) c.url = v.url;
  }
  let list = [...map.values()].filter((c) => c.seconds >= minSec);
  const q = query.trim().toLowerCase();
  if (q) list = list.filter((c) => c.name.toLowerCase().includes(q));
  list.sort((a, b) => {
    if (sort === "plays")
      return b.plays - a.plays || b.seconds - a.seconds || b.visits - a.visits;
    if (sort === "visits")
      return b.visits - a.visits || b.seconds - a.seconds || b.plays - a.plays;
    return b.seconds - a.seconds || b.plays - a.plays || b.visits - a.visits;
  });
  const maxSec = Math.max(0, ...list.map((c) => c.seconds));
  list = list.map((c) => ({
    ...c,
    barPct: maxSec > 0 ? Math.round((c.seconds / maxSec) * 100) : 0,
  }));
  return {
    list,
    totals: {
      count: list.length,
      visits: list.reduce((s, c) => s + c.visits, 0),
      seconds: list.reduce((s, c) => s + c.seconds, 0),
      withTime: list.filter((c) => c.seconds > 0).length,
    },
    recent: [...visits].sort((a, b) => b.timestamp - a.timestamp).slice(0, 12),
  };
}

export function channelDetailVM(name, plays, visits) {
  const key = name.trim().toLowerCase();
  const mine = plays.filter(
    (p) => (p.artist || "").trim().toLowerCase() === key,
  );
  const myVisits = visits
    .filter((v) => (v.name || v.channelKey || "").trim().toLowerCase() === key)
    .sort((a, b) => b.timestamp - a.timestamp);
  let seconds = 0,
    avatar = "",
    url = "";
  for (const p of mine) {
    seconds += p.seconds || 0;
    if (!avatar && p.artwork) avatar = p.artwork;
    if (!url && p.channelUrl) url = p.channelUrl;
  }
  for (const v of myVisits) {
    if (v.avatar) avatar = v.avatar;
    if (v.url) url = v.url;
  }
  const dedupe = (items) => {
    const m = new Map();
    for (const it of items) {
      const k = getGroupKey(it);
      const cur = m.get(k);
      if (!cur || it.timestamp > cur.timestamp) m.set(k, it);
    }
    return [...m.values()]
      .sort((a, b) => b.timestamp - a.timestamp)
      .slice(0, 12);
  };
  return {
    name: mine[0]?.artist || myVisits[0]?.name || name,
    avatar,
    url,
    plays: mine.length,
    seconds,
    visits: myVisits.length,
    topTracks: aggregateTracks(mine).slice(0, 20),
    shorts: dedupe(mine.filter((p) => p.contentType === "short")),
    lives: dedupe(mine.filter((p) => p.contentType === "live")),
    recent: dedupe(mine),
    recentVisits: myVisits.slice(0, 12),
    ids: aggregateTracks(mine)
      .map((t) => t.videoId)
      .filter(Boolean),
  };
}

export function likesPageVM(inter, searches, plays) {
  const likes = inter
    .filter((i) => i.type === "like")
    .sort((a, b) => b.timestamp - a.timestamp);
  const subs = inter
    .filter((i) => i.type === "subscribe")
    .sort((a, b) => b.timestamp - a.timestamp);
  const groups = new Map();
  for (const s of searches) {
    const k = s.normalized || (s.query || "").toLowerCase();
    const g = groups.get(k) || {
      query: s.query,
      count: 0,
      lastTs: 0,
      url: s.url,
      source: s.source,
    };
    g.count += 1;
    if (s.timestamp > g.lastTs) {
      g.lastTs = s.timestamp;
      if (s.url) g.url = s.url;
    }
    groups.set(k, g);
  }
  const searchGroups = [...groups.values()].sort((a, b) => b.lastTs - a.lastTs);
  const collage = likes
    .map((l) => l.artwork)
    .filter(Boolean)
    .slice(0, 4);
  const ids = [...new Set(likes.map((l) => l.videoId).filter(Boolean))];
  const likedKeys = new Set();
  for (const l of likes) {
    if (l.videoId) likedKeys.add(l.videoId);
    if (l.targetKey) likedKeys.add(l.targetKey);
  }
  let seconds = 0;
  for (const p of plays)
    if (likedKeys.has(p.videoId) || likedKeys.has(getGroupKey(p)))
      seconds += p.seconds || 0;
  const oldest = likes.length ? likes[likes.length - 1].timestamp : null;
  return {
    likes,
    subs,
    searchGroups,
    collage,
    ids,
    oldest,
    totals: {
      likes: likes.length,
      subs: subs.length,
      searches: searches.length,
      groups: searchGroups.length,
      seconds,
    },
  };
}

/* ===================== v2.7 ===================== */

export function kpiMicroVM(plays, prevPlays) {
  const days = 7;
  const base = new Date();
  base.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
  const start = base.getTime() - (days - 1) * 86400000;
  const days7 = Array.from({ length: days }, () => ({
    seconds: 0,
    plays: 0,
    active: false,
  }));
  const hours = new Array(24).fill(0);
  let loops = 0;
  for (const p of plays) {
    const d = new Date(p.timestamp);
    hours[d.getHours()] += p.seconds || 0;
    if (p.isLoop) loops += 1;
    d.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
    const idx = Math.round((d.getTime() - start) / 86400000);
    if (idx >= 0 && idx < days) {
      days7[idx].seconds += p.seconds || 0;
      days7[idx].plays += 1;
      days7[idx].active = true;
    }
  }
  const unique = aggregateTracks(plays).length;
  const prevUnique = prevPlays.length ? aggregateTracks(prevPlays).length : 0;
  return {
    days7,
    hours,
    loops,
    plays: plays.length,
    unique,
    prevUnique,
    loopsRatio: plays.length ? loops / plays.length : 0,
    uniqueRatio: prevUnique
      ? Math.min(1, unique / prevUnique)
      : plays.length
        ? Math.min(1, unique / plays.length)
        : 0,
  };
}

export function prevHoursVM(prevPlays) {
  const h = new Array(24).fill(0);
  for (const p of prevPlays)
    h[new Date(p.timestamp).getHours()] += p.seconds || 0;
  return h;
}

export function yearHeatVM(plays, year) {
  const jan1 = new Date(year, 0, 1);
  const dec31 = new Date(year, 11, 31);
  const startPad = (jan1.getDay() + 6) % 7;
  const byDay = new Map();
  for (const p of plays) {
    const d = new Date(p.timestamp);
    if (d.getFullYear() !== year) continue;
    const k = toDateKey(d);
    byDay.set(k, (byDay.get(k) || 0) + (p.seconds || 0));
  }
  const cells = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < startPad; i++) cells.push(null);
  for (let d = new Date(jan1); d <= dec31; d.setDate(d.getDate() + 1)) {
    const sec = byDay.get(toDateKey(d)) || 0;
    const level =
      sec >= 7200 ? 4 : sec >= 2700 ? 3 : sec >= 900 ? 2 : sec > 0 ? 1 : 0;
    cells.push({ key: toDateKey(d), level });
  }
  return {
    cells,
    startPad,
    total: [...byDay.values()].reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0),
  };
}

export function insightsVM(plays, prevPlays) {
  const out = [];
  if (!plays.length) return out;
  const byDay = new Map();
  const hours = new Array(24).fill(0);
  let total = 0;
  for (const p of plays) {
    const s = p.seconds || 0;
    total += s;
    hours[new Date(p.timestamp).getHours()] += s;
    const k = toDateKey(new Date(p.timestamp));
    byDay.set(k, (byDay.get(k) || 0) + s);
  }
  const best = [...byDay.entries()].sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1])[0];
  if (best && best[1] > 0) {
    const [y, m, d] = best[0].split("-").map(Number);
    const label = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("es-AR", {
      weekday: "long",
      day: "numeric",
      month: "long",
    }).format(new Date(y, m - 1, d));
    out.push({
      icon: "trophy",
      tone: "annotate",
      text: `Tu mejor día fue <strong>${esc(label)}</strong> con <strong>${formatDuration(best[1])}</strong>.`,
      action: { type: "calendar", y, m: m - 1, d },
    });
  }
  if (total > 0) {
    const eve = hours[19] + hours[20] + hours[21] + hours[22];
    const pct = Math.round((eve / total) * 100);
    if (pct >= 10) {
      out.push({
        icon: "moon",
        tone: "compare",
        text: `Concentrás el <strong>${pct}%</strong> de tu tiempo entre <strong>19:00 y 22:00</strong>.`,
        action: { type: "hour", h: 19 },
      });
    }
  }
  const days = [...byDay.keys()].sort();
  let maxRun = 0,
    run = 0,
    prevT = null;
  for (const k of days) {
    const t = Date.parse(`${k}T00:00:00Z`);
    if (prevT != null && t - prevT === 86400000) run += 1;
    else run = 1;
    if (run > maxRun) maxRun = run;
    prevT = t;
  }
  const cur = computeStreak(plays);
  out.push({
    icon: "flame",
    tone: "ok",
    text: `Llevás <strong>${cur} ${cur === 1 ? "día" : "días"}</strong> de racha; tu récord del período es <strong>${maxRun}</strong>.`,
    action: { type: "calendar" },
  });
  const loops = plays.filter((p) => p.isLoop).length;
  if (loops > 0) {
    out.push({
      icon: "loop",
      tone: "brand",
      text: `Repetiste <strong>${loops}</strong> reproducciones: el <strong>${Math.round((loops / plays.length) * 100)}%</strong> del total.`,
      action: { type: "none" },
    });
  }
  if (prevPlays.length) {
    const ps = prevPlays.reduce((s, p) => s + (p.seconds || 0), 0);
    if (ps > 0) {
      const pct = Math.round(((total - ps) / ps) * 100);
      out.push({
        icon: pct >= 0 ? "peak" : "chart",
        tone: pct >= 0 ? "ok" : "bad",
        text: `${pct >= 0 ? "Subiste" : "Bajaste"} <strong>${Math.abs(pct)}%</strong> de tiempo vs el período anterior.`,
        action: { type: "none" },
      });
    }
  } else {
    out.push({
      icon: "info",
      tone: "muted",
      text: "Este es tu <strong>primer período con datos</strong>: todavía no hay comparación anterior.",
      action: { type: "none" },
    });
  }
  const byArtist = new Map();
  for (const p of plays) {
    const a = p.artist || "Desconocido";
    byArtist.set(a, (byArtist.get(a) || 0) + (p.seconds || 0));
  }
  const topA = [...byArtist.entries()].sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1])[0];
  if (topA && total > 0) {
    const pct = Math.round((topA[1] / total) * 100);
    if (pct >= 15) {
      out.push({
        icon: "mic",
        tone: "brand",
        text: `El <strong>${pct}%</strong> de tu tiempo es de <strong>${esc(topA[0])}</strong>.`,
        action: { type: "people", name: topA[0] },
      });
    }
  }
  return out.slice(0, 5);
}

export { formatDuration, toDateKey };
